# crush.lu customer-experience review — 2026-10-05

> **DELETE AFTER TRIAGE.** One-time, autonomous review of the five core products, produced from this repository only (code, templates, copy, tests, and pages rendered against a local SQLite database). It is not a backlog and it is not part of the product. Triage what you want into issues, then delete this file.

**Scope:** Speed Dating · Quiz Night · Crush Connect · Crush Coach + Coach Panel · cross-cutting journey (signup, onboarding, verification, notifications and emails, cancellation and Crush Credit, account/privacy, mobile/PWA).  
**Base:** `main` at `ec37261` ("Refresh event guides with Quiz Night and check-in-first Speed Dating (#1174)").  
**No production data, no analytics, no secrets, no vault.** Where data would settle a question the text says `needs data: <metric>`; nothing here is a usage number.

## 1. How this was produced, and how far to trust it

All work below is model output. It was produced in four stages, each by separate agents:

| Stage | What happened |
|---|---|
| Setup | Installed deps, built the generated assets (`python scripts/build_assets.py`), migrated a local SQLite DB and seeded sample events, members, coaches and a Connect lobby. Open PRs and issues were read to build a do-not-touch list (Appendix B). |
| 1. Review | One reviewer per product mapped the journey (screens, emails, push, WhatsApp), walked three personas (first-timer, returning member, edge case) against a 12-point checklist, and rendered real pages in EN/DE/FR where it could. |
| 2. Challenge | 95 Blocks/Hurts findings were each given to a verifier whose job was to disprove them: re-render, trace the code, check other screens, check open PRs/issues. |
| 3. Synthesis | One agent turned confirmed findings into proposals, a top 10, a needs-data list, and a shortlist of safe fixes. |
| 4. Fix PRs | Up to four draft PRs for copy-only/template-only changes, each in its own worktree (section 5). |

**Results of the challenge stage**

- 123 findings in total; 95 (all Blocks/Hurts) were challenged. **90 confirmed, 5 plausible, 0 rejected.** The remaining 28 Polish items were not challenged.
- Zero outright rejections does **not** mean the reviewers were right about everything. The verifiers lowered the severity of 17 findings, marked the reviewer's proposed AFTER copy as unsafe or incomplete for 30, and rated 5 as only plausible; 39 of 95 findings had at least one of those three outcomes. Many of the rest carry smaller corrections (a wrong line, a narrower scope, a sub-claim disproved), recorded in Appendix A. Where the verifier corrected a proposal, the corrected wording in this report is the verifier's, not the reviewer's original.
- Severity after challenge: **3 Blocks, 78 Hurts, 42 Polish.** The three remaining Blocks are SD-01 (stale checkout claim, traced in code, not reproduced), CC-01 (Premium "first month free" vs the charge) and CO-01 (rejected at the door, merged with XC-01).
- 5 challenged findings depend on `select_for_update` behaviour and are **untested on Postgres** (SQLite ignores it); they are marked in Appendix A.

**Limits that matter when weighing this**

- The verifiers are the same kind of model as the reviewers, so shared blind spots are possible. A human who uses these screens will see things neither pass did.
- Everything was rendered on SQLite with seeded data, behind a WSGI dev server (no WebSockets; the Quiz Night live flow was driven with Channels' test communicator). The sandbox browser did not load Tailwind CSS, so mobile layout, tap targets and contrast were mostly **not** measured (Section 7).
- SumUp, LuxID, push delivery, WhatsApp, wallet passes and email delivery were not exercised; those findings are code-reading.
- No finding states a number of affected members. Every frequency is a `needs data` question (Section 6).

**Security/privacy item for triage (not a copy finding, not implemented).** `GET /api/quiz/<event_id>/display-data/` answers anonymously with guests' display names, roles, table numbers and photo URLs whenever `QuizEvent.display_token` is blank. Blank is the default and the field's help text says "Leave blank for unrestricted access" (`crush_lu/views_quiz.py:420-431`, `crush_lu/models/quiz.py:187`), so this is by design for the projector, but event ids are sequential and these are attendees of a dating event. A verifier flagged it; I confirmed the anonymous 200 with `curl` against the local server. Needs a security/privacy decision (proposal QN-B1).

## 2. Top 10 customer pain points


Repo HEAD `ec37261`, 2026-10-05. Ranked by impact on, in this order: registration completion, no-shows, repeat attendance, trust. Severities are the post-verifier ones. Each paragraph describes what a real member sees, using only copy and behaviour I could read or render; frequencies are unknown and are listed in Section 6. "P" = item ids in `proposals.md`; "Phase 4" = bundle in the fix PR descriptions (Section 5).

Merged duplicates: CO-01 + XC-01 (same rejected-state chain); XC-10 + SD-06 + SD-25 (waitlist promise); SD-10 + XC-08 (credit visibility); QN-18 + XC-16 + CC-02 + CC-18 + SD-04 + CO-20 (untranslated DE/FR); QN-16 + dashboard Join Quiz; CO-12 + CC-12 (silent Premium redirect); SD-09 + XC-12 (what is promised before commitment).

---

### 1. "How much will this cost me, and what do I get back?" is answered too late or never (Hurts)
**Findings:** SD-09, SD-05, SD-10, SD-11, SD-12, XC-08, XC-02 (with SD-13 for the unpaid applicant). **Proposals:** P SD-M2, XC-M2, SD-B1; Phase 4 P4-1 (SD-11 caveat).

A member who registers for a paid evening sees "Event Fee: EUR 15.50" and a button; the refund-and-credit rule appears only after they click Pay (the SumUp widget) or when they cancel. After a cancellation they are told "ready to use on any Crush.lu event" with no date, and no page anywhere shows their balance or expiry; a late cancellation promises "7.75 EUR back as Crush Credit" without saying a half-ticket credit cannot buy a seat. A curated invitee is told "Your place is held for you" with no pay-by time (none exists; unpaid seats are never released), and a member whose group fell apart after they paid is told on the site that "No payment was recorded" while an email says credit was returned. Deleting a profile quietly voids unspent credit and paid seats. It matters because credit is the only form in which money comes back, and members who cannot see it assume it is lost. **Why #1:** every paid registrant meets it, at the moment of commitment and again at cancellation.

### 2. Getting in: signup, verification link, password reset, wrong language (Hurts)
**Findings:** XC-09, XC-06, XC-15, XC-16, XC-22, XC-21. **Proposals:** P XC-QW1, XC-QW3, XC-B1; Phase 4 P4-1 (XC-09), P4-2 (XC-16).

After signing up, the first-timer reaches "Check Your Inbox". If the link has expired, "Request a New Link" lands on the same page saying "We've sent a verification link..." although nothing was sent, with no validity period and no support address. A French signup on an English-default phone gets a French mail, then an English waiting page and English login, and the language the member signed up in is never stored; the verification mail greets a lower-cased generated username ("Hi elodie"; XC-22, not adversarially verified). After verifying they re-type their password and arrive on a dashboard showing "Step 1 of 4" instead of the guided welcome. The password-reset confirmation is headed "Check for updates" in DE and FR. **Why #2:** it is the gate in front of the whole product, and it is where non-English speakers first meet English.

### 3. Waitlist and unpaid seats say things that are not true (Hurts)
**Findings:** SD-06, XC-10, XC-11, SD-25, SD-07, SD-24, SD-18. **Proposals:** P SD-QW1, SD-M2, SD-M3; Phase 4 P4-1 (SD-06) and P4-3 (XC-10).

Right after joining a full paid event the member sees "You're on the Waitlist! #1" and, one line below, a button "Pay with Card (15.50 EUR)"; tapping it fails with "This registration cannot be paid for in its current state." My Events shows a ticket icon for the waitlist card that leads to "We couldn't find that page". The waitlist email promises "you'll be automatically moved to confirmed", but on a paid event promotion creates an unpaid seat and a "Payment required" email, with no push or bell, no named deadline, and the generic "Your Spot Is Reserved" wording (also used for curated invitations). The day-before reminder tells an unpaid member "You're registered" with no payment line, while the door admits unpaid seats. **Why #3:** each of these is on the path from interest to a paid seat, and the waitlist screen shows a button designed to fail.

### 4. "I owe money and cannot pay" (Blocks, SD-01; frequency unknown)
**Findings:** SD-01 (related SD-12). **Proposals:** P SD-M1.

A member who taps "Pay with Card" and hits a slow or ambiguous SumUp answer is told "Unable to initiate payment at the moment"; every retry then gets "A payment checkout is already being prepared. Please wait and try again." The verifier reproduced a claim 3 days old still blocking, found nothing in request traffic or on any timer that retires it (`cleanup_event_checkout_claims --apply` is run by hand), and the same 409 blocks the Crush Credit button. The toast offers no support address. For a curated evening it also stalls the group. **Why #4:** it is the only verified state where a willing payer cannot proceed and nobody is alerted; ranked below 1-3 only because how often it happens is unknown (needs data).

### 5. German and French members meet English, or the wrong words, at the most sensitive moments (Hurts)
**Findings:** SD-04, CC-02, CC-18, QN-18, XC-16, CO-20, plus S-01 found during synthesis (and XC-19/XC-20/SD-20 as unverified polish). **Proposals:** P SD-QW2, QN-QW2, CC-QW3, CO-QW3, XC-QW3, XC-M1; Phase 4 P4-2 and P4-4.

On `/fr/` every registration page and ticket shows a link "Retour à la connexion" (= Back to login) where "Back to Event" should be. After an event the My Crush! page is half English in DE/FR; behind the empty entries sit fuzzy ones that are wrong (a "Quiz created" toast, "One match per week"), so simply un-fuzzying would ship nonsense. The two required Crush Connect consent boxes, the card button ("Answer"), the "I feel unsafe" removal form and the "This person is now hidden" confirmation are English. On a curated event page the banner "Your application is in!" and, after applying, "Application Received!" are English because those msgids have no `.po` entry at all; so is the footer link "Cookie Settings" on every page and the toast "Unable to initiate payment at the moment". Coaches working in DE/FR get an English "Photo mismatch" button and its destructive confirm. **Why #5:** a trilingual product loses trust each time the language drops, and the cause (no gate on missing msgids, #1154 counts only fuzzy ones) is systemic.

### 6. The information a guest needs to show up on time is wrong or missing (Hurts)
**Findings:** SD-03, SD-14, SD-21, QN-06, QN-15, SD-24. **Proposals:** P SD-M3, QN-QW1, QN-M3, SD-B2; Phase 4 P4-3 (QN-15).

The day-before push and the Apple/Google wallet ticket print the UTC time: measured on one event, push "21:28", wallet "09:28 PM", site and email "23:28" (two hours apart in summer; late events can show the wrong date too), and the pass labels are English with a 12-hour clock for DE/FR members. A member running late, or who cannot come, is told "contact your coach" (English in DE/FR, and a first-timer has no coach and no coach contact is shown); the guide promises "Running late? Contact the organiser listed on your event page" and no organiser is listed. A Quiz Night guest is never told in the confirmation or reminder that the evening needs a charged phone and that check-in assigns the table; one who scans the projector QR before the coach scanned their ticket gets "We couldn't find that page". **Why #6:** these drive no-shows and a bad first ten minutes; the time bug is a plain code defect.

### 7. Rejected at the door: accused, looped, and told to browse events (Blocks CO-01; Hurts XC-01)
**Findings:** CO-01 + XC-01 (merged), CO-03. **Proposals:** P CO-M1, CO-QW1; Phase 4 P4-3 (CO-03).

A member whose photo did not match at the door sees, on the profile page, "Your profile does not comply with our Terms of Service and has not been approved. It cannot be resubmitted." The real reason (photo mismatch) went only to an email and bell. The dashboard says "Profile not approved - Please contact support" directly above "No upcoming events yet / Browse upcoming events" and a donation card; the event page says "Finish your profile to register" with a button that bounces back to the rejected page; the rejection email advises "You may create a new profile" although the account cannot. A coach may re-verify a rejected profile at the door, but the member can no longer register for an event where that could happen. **Why #7:** the highest severity in the review (Blocks) and a false accusation, but a small population.

### 8. Premium: the advertised price and the charged price differ (Blocks, CC-01)
**Findings:** CC-01, CC-12, CO-12. **Proposals:** P CC-M1.

"Go Premium — first month free" appears on the dashboard, the Connect marketing page, the coach-pick explainer and the verification card. Checkout charges the full monthly fee immediately and tokenises the card for recurring billing; the price first appears after a coach is chosen; "Cancel anytime" has no self-service cancel for an active membership. Today most members are redirected silently to a page about something else (`PREMIUM_REDIRECTS_TO_BETA`), so exposure is limited to selected testers; at launch it is everyone. **Why #8:** the highest-trust moment (money) and the likeliest source of refund and chargeback requests; ranked below 1-7 because the beta redirect limits exposure today, and it drops to Hurts if a free month is granted outside the repo (needs data).

### 9. After the event: the follow-up promise is invisible, fragile, or inexact (Hurts)
**Findings:** SD-08, CO-09, CO-15, CC-09, QN-23 (with SD-04, CO-07, CO-08). **Proposals:** P SD-QW3, CO-QW2, CO-M3, CC-B2; Phase 4 P4-3 (SD-08).

The 24 h recap email is the only reminder that the connection window closes in 12-24 hours, but members with no activity get "It was lovely to have you. The next event is around the corner" and no mention of My Crush!. The product's promise is "Your Crush Coach will call you within 48 hours", yet the only nudge to the coach is a push that is stamped delivered when no coach device is opted in, behind a toggle labelled "Screening Reminders" that coaches would naturally mute; the coach "My Members" list only shows members the coach approved through the retired review, so door-verified members do not appear, and the member is never told who their coach is. "Your crush stays completely private — they are never notified" is literally false because the recipient's own coach contacts them. In Connect, two members who both confirm they met reach "Say hi next time you see them" and nothing else. **Why #9:** repeat attendance and the privacy promise both depend on a human step that nothing tracks.

### 10. Trust: what "verified" means, what the door checks, and who can see my data (Hurts)
**Findings:** XC-07, XC-14, CC-14, CO-14, XC-12, XC-13, CO-02, CC-04, QN-21. **Proposals:** P XC-QW2, XC-M3, QN-B1, CO-M2, CO-B1; Phase 4 P4-1 (XC-07).

A first-timer is unsure what "verified" means and the product does not help: after submitting the longest form on the site the nav says "Profile Status: Not Submitted" on every page; the dashboard says "You're verified" and "Get verified to join Crush Connect" together; the door notification says "Your profile is approved ... You can now register for events" to someone already registered. "Valid ID (for age verification)" appears only in two emails after registering, while onboarding says being verified "is all it takes"; a member with no profile photo is checked in but not verified, and the dashboard still says nothing is needed. The photo step never says who sees photos, and the Connect consent lists fewer items than the card actually shows. Separately, `GET /api/quiz/<event_id>/display-data/` answers **anonymously** with display names, roles, tables and photo URLs of Quiz Night attendees whenever the optional PIN is blank (the default), and no page tells guests a venue screen shows them (**privacy review needed; do not implement**). **Why #10:** this is the top-of-funnel anxiety plus the one confirmed exposure of attendee data; most fixes wait on owner decisions and on PR #1163.

---

#### Also considered (not in the ten, see proposals)
* Quiz host panel mistakes (End is terminal, ghost tables, no undo, silent buttons offline): QN-01/07/08/19 (P QN-M1).
* Connect Week silence (acceptance is a bell only, no review/chat notifications) and the one weekly request's undisclosed rules: CC-10, CC-07 (P CC-B1, CC-M2).
* Consent and preference switches that do not do what they say (Marketing toggle gates no send, WhatsApp copy over-promises, "Pause all emails" silences the paid-seat confirmation): XC-03/04/05 (P XC-B2).

## 3. Per-product journey maps and findings

Severity is the post-challenge value. `Verdict` is the verifier's. IDs resolve to Appendix A (full evidence, quoted copy, BEFORE/AFTER proposals, verifier notes). `Copy-only` means the fix is template or `.po` text with no model, migration, payment, auth, permission or logic change.

### 3.1 Speed Dating

**25 findings:** 1 Blocks · 14 Hurts · 10 Polish.

#### Journey map


Reviewer: product 1 (sd). Date of review: 2026-10-05. Repo state: commit `ec37261` (#1174) on top of `main`.
Method: every step marked **[R]** was rendered through the Django test client against the local dev SQLite DB
(EN, and DE/FR where noted) with `sd_`-prefixed users/events; **[C]** = traced in code/templates only.
Local reviewer data: events 25 (curated, paid 15 EUR, selection stage), 26 (curated, applications open), 27 (direct, max 2),
28 (direct, starts in 20h), 29 (direct, past), 32 (direct, live now); users `sd_m1..7`, `sd_f1..7`, `sd_wait`, `sd_cancel`,
`sd_attended`, `sd_age_out`, `sd_nolang`, `sd_unverified`, `sd_pay`, `sd_de`, `sd_fr`.
The curated flow (apply -> generate -> approve -> invite -> pay -> check-in -> lock) was driven through the real admin actions
(`generate_curated_groups`, `approve_curated_groups`, `invite_approved_curated_groups`, `lock_curated_groups`); SumUp itself is
not configured locally so the card widget and capture handler were read, not exercised.
Personas: P1 first-timer (28-45, phone, anxious), P2 returning member (speed), P3 edge case (unverified / waitlisted / cancelled /
payment failed / wrong language / screen reader).
Findings are referenced as SD-nn (see Appendix A).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### A. Discovery

###### A1. Event list `/{lang}/events/` [R]
- Template/view: `event_list.html`, `views_events.event_list`. Header buttons "Discover Speed Dating" / "Discover Quiz Night" (added by #1174).
- Cards: type chip, price ("EUR 15.50" / "Free"), ages, event languages, "Only N spots left", and for curated "Curated groups · applications open".
- Language: i18n_patterns + modeltranslation for title/description (local seeded events only have EN, so DE/FR fall back to EN - seed artefact, not reported).
- P1: "Curated" is unexplained jargon on the card (SD-19). Price shown, refund rule not (SD-09).
- P3 (screen reader): cards are links with text; no issue found.

###### A2. Format guide `/{lang}/speed-dating/` [R, EN/DE/FR]
- Template/view: `speed_dating_guide.html`, `views_static.speed_dating_guide`. Promises: coach check-in, "Chat for a few minutes, then change partners at the coach's signal", "No public introduction round", "Arrive at the advertised time", "Running late? Contact the organiser listed on your event page", curated = "apply for free first. A place is not guaranteed".
- Linked from the events-list header, how-it-works and the Quiz guide. **Not linked from the speed-dating event page, the application page, or any email** (SD-02).
- P1: this is the best anxiety-reducing page in the product, but a member deciding on a concrete event never sees it.
- Contradictions with the rest of the product: SD-02 (voting box), SD-14 ("organiser listed on your event page"), SD-24 (arrival time).

###### A3. Event detail `/{lang}/events/<id>/` [R] - `event_detail.html`, `views_events.event_detail`
States rendered/traced (one template, many branches):
| State | What the member sees (EN quote) | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Anonymous | "Log in to apply" / "or sign up to register for this event." | canton only, exact address hidden |
| Eligible, curated, open | "Apply for a place - no payment now / 1. Apply by ...; applying does not reserve a place. 2. After applications close, we form groups around mutual preferences. 3. If selected for a viable group, you will be invited to pay to confirm." | #1175 covers the application-page line; #1126 shipped the CTA |
| Eligible, direct, seats left | "Register for This Event" | no refund/credit rule on page (SD-09) |
| Direct, full | "0 spots left", "Spots remaining: 0", "Almost full!", CTA "Join Waitlist" | SD-25 |
| Age out of range | normal "Apply for This Event" CTA, no explanation | SD-23 |
| Language mismatch | "Language Requirement ... don't match your profile" + button "Update Profile Languages" **and** the Apply CTA | SD-23 |
| Applied | "Your application is in! ... will invite you to pay only if you are selected ... Selection is not guaranteed." + "Withdraw Application" | no decision date (SD-13 family); "few applicants match your preferences; widening them helps" (SD-15) |
| Selected, unpaid (`pending`) | "Payment due - EUR 15.00 / Your spot is reserved. Complete payment below to confirm it." + "Your group is provisional ... The group can still change until it is locked" + Pay with Card + View My Ticket + Cancel Registration | no pay-by time (SD-05), no refund/credit rule (SD-09) |
| Waitlist | "You're on the waitlist - We'll email you if a spot opens up. No payment is needed until then." | no position, no timeline (SD-25) |
| Confirmed | "You're in! We've sent a confirmation email with all the event details." + calendar + ticket | |
| Live | red banner "This event is happening now / Show your ticket at the door to check in." (also for already-checked-in members) | SD-21 |
| Attended, group locked | "Your group is final. ... Your tables: Round 1: table 1, seat A ..." plus "Access Voting Lobby" | SD-02 |
| Past + never selected | still "Your application is in!" | SD-13 |
| Past + attended | "View Attendees & Connections" | SD-17 |
- Language handling: gettext for chrome; description/title via modeltranslation; mobile sticky CTA bar (`eventStickyCta`, `core.js`) mirrors the primary anchor.
- Voting box ("How The Event Works - Interactive Activity Voting ...") renders on every event with `enable_activity_voting` (all seeded speed-dating events and the September command output): SD-02. DE shows one untranslated English line, FR shows "Sélection interactive de parcelle" (SD-20).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### B. Application / registration

###### B1. Form `/{lang}/events/<id>/register/` [R] - `event_register.html`, `EventPreferenceForm`
- Fields: dietary (only when `has_food_component`), plus-one (only when allowed), age confirmation (only without profile), gender (only when pooled and unset), and for speed dating "Who would you like to meet?" / "Preferred age range" / "Preferred language at the table".
- Reassurance present: "Used only by the organiser team to compose the tables - never shown to other members."
- Prefill: Connect membership -> profile preferences -> blank (P2 does not have to retype, unless they tuned per event). Crossed age range is swapped (good).
- Curated page: "Price if selected: EUR 15.00" and "Applying is free and does not reserve a place..." (#1175 area). Direct page: "Event Fee: EUR 15.50" + "Confirm Registration" - **no hint that the next screen asks for money, no deadline, no refund rule** (SD-09, SD-05).
- Full direct event: banner "Event is Full - You will be added to the waitlist." but Event Fee still shown, no "no charge unless a place opens" (SD-25).
- Rate limit `@ratelimit("user", "5/h", POST)`: valid-and-invalid submits both count; not an issue unless a member keeps correcting (needs data: 429s on this route).
- The form never says that narrow preferences reduce the chance of being placed (SD-15).

###### B2. Submit
- HTMX swap -> `_event_registration_success.html`; non-HTMX -> redirect to dashboard + flash.
  - Curated: "Application Received! The organiser team composes the group before the event. We'll let you know whether you have a place - you don't need to pay anything yet." (no date for the answer).
  - Direct paid: "Your Spot Is Reserved! Complete your payment to confirm your registration. We've emailed you the details." + Pay with Card (15.50 EUR).
  - **Waitlist: "You're on the Waitlist! #1 ..." + the same Pay with Card button** (SD-06).
- Email: direct paid -> `send_event_payment_pending_notification`; direct free -> `send_event_registration_confirmation`; waitlist -> `send_event_waitlist_notification`; curated application -> none by design.
- Admin side: Coach/admin actions generate -> approve -> invite (`admin/events.py` ~1280-1340); invitations are queued (`CuratedGroupNotification`) and delivered in batches of 10 (SD-16).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### C. Selection, payment, waitlist, cancellation

###### C1. Selected / not selected (curated) [R]
- Selected: registration `applied -> pending`; email via `curated_group_notifications._deliver_selection_notice` -> `send_event_payment_pending_notification` (the **generic direct-event "Your Spot Is Reserved / Payment required to confirm" mail**, no group, no deadline - SD-07). EN/DE/FR rendered ("Salut Claire !" in FR is #1166).
- Not selected: `curated_group_reserve.html` ("Your application stays in the pool ... No payment was taken") - good copy, FR rendered (EN/DE read in templates/.po). Withdrawal notice `curated_group_withdrawal.html` and remedy `curated_group_payment_remedy.html` exist.
- On site: not-selected applicant keeps seeing "Your application is in!" (and after the event too - SD-13).
- Push/in-app/WhatsApp: **none** for selection or promotion (SD-18).

###### C2. Payment [C + R of surrounding pages]
- Entry points: event detail "Pay with Card (15.00 EUR)" / "Pay with Crush Credit" (only when balance >= fee), My Events card, ticket-page "Pay now" strip, payment email "Pay Now" (links to the event page, 2 more taps).
- `sumupCheckoutButton` (`core.js` ~9341) POSTs `/payments/sumup/create-event-checkout/<reg>/` (language-neutral URL) -> widget page `/payments/sumup/widget/<checkout>/` (`sumup_widget.html`): order summary + **cancellation policy note** + 3DS polling + failure reporting -> `/payments/sumup/return/` -> flash + redirect to event page.
- Return copy: paid "Payment completed successfully! Thank you."; unpaid "Your payment is still pending or was not completed. Your spot is reserved - you can retry payment below."
- Failure surfaces: toast with the server JSON `error` or the generic "An error occurred launching payment. Please try again."; stale claim -> 409 "A payment checkout is already being prepared. Please wait and try again." (SD-01); SDK load failure "Failed to load payment module. Please refresh or contact support." (no contact link).
- Capture-time remedies for curated (group no longer certified): credit + remedy email, registration reverts to `applied` (SD-12). Capture after member cancellation / event cancellation: credit + email (handled).
- Not exercised (no SumUp credentials locally): widget, webhook, polling. Postgres-only lock behaviour untested (SQLite ignores `select_for_update`).
- P3 (payment failed): can retry from event page; the "Your spot is reserved" phrasing repeats on retry although there is no hold deadline (SD-05).

###### C3. Waitlist [R]
- Join: success screen (position #n) + `event_waitlist.html` ("If someone cancels, you'll be automatically moved to confirmed" - not true for paid events, where promotion lands on `pending`; SD-25).
- Promotion: `_promote_from_waitlist` on cancel -> `send_event_payment_pending_notification` (same generic mail; no "you moved off the waitlist" wording; SD-07). No push/bell (SD-18).
- My Events shows a ticket icon for waitlisted entries that 404s (SD-06).
- Position is not shown on the event page afterwards (SD-25).

###### C4. Cancel / withdraw `/events/<id>/cancel/` [R] - `event_cancel.html`
- Preview uses `cancellation_outcome`: paid > 48h = "You'll get 15.50 EUR back as Crush Credit, ready to use on any Crush.lu event." + policy note ("Cancel before ... for full Crush Credit. After that there's no refund, but you get 50% back as Crush Credit if someone takes your seat and pays before the event."); paid < 48h = "No refund: the event starts in less than 48 hours ..."; unpaid = "No payment was recorded for this registration, so no Crush Credit is due."
- Applicants / waitlisted / unpaid invitees get the same page: "Cancel Event Registration", "Keep my seat" although they hold no seat (SD-22).
- Expiry (six months) and the all-or-nothing rule are not stated here (SD-10, SD-11).
- After start: flash "This event has already started. If you can't make it, contact your coach." - DE/FR msgstr empty (English shown) and first-timers have no coach (SD-14).
- Emails: `event_cancellation.html` ("keeps the applicable credit expiry" - no date).

###### C5. Crush Credit [R/C]
- No member-visible balance or expiry anywhere (only the pay button appears when balance >= fee); expiry-reminder command is not on a timer (SD-10); partial credits unusable alone (SD-11).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### D. Reminders and day-of

###### D1. Reminders [C + R of email]
- Schedule (`azure-functions/hybrid-maintenance/function_app.py`): `EventReminders` hourly at :35 between 09 and 20 UTC -> `/api/admin/event-reminders/` -> `send_event_reminders` default `--days 1`: all seat-holding registrations (`pending`, `confirmed`, `attended`) for events **on the next calendar day**, stamped once (`reminder_sent_at`). No same-day "starts in 2h" sweep is scheduled although the command supports `--hours-before`.
- Channels: in-app bell row + web push + native iOS/Android push + email (`event_reminder.html`). **No WhatsApp** for events (WhatsApp is OTP + campaigns only).
- Content: web push "Event Tomorrow: {title} / Don't forget! {title} starts at {HH:MM}" - **time is UTC** (SD-03); native push "Event reminder: {title} / Make sure you're ready - check the details."; email lists date/time/location, the full marketing description, "Valid ID", "Arrive 10-15 minutes early", cancel link. Unpaid `pending` members get the same "You're registered" mail with no payment prompt, no ticket link (SD-24).
- Language: email in profile `preferred_language`; push via `user_language_context`. DE reminder shows one untranslated English bullet ("Be yourself and relax"; SD-20).

###### D2. Ticket / door `/events/<id>/ticket/` [R] - `event_ticket.html`, `views_ticket.event_ticket`
- Seat-holding statuses only (`pending`, `confirmed`, `attended`): applicants and waitlisted get 404 (correct, but the waitlist is still linked - SD-06).
- Content: name, QR (server-rendered SVG), "Ticket number #n", "Show this QR code to the coach at the event entrance", payment-due strip if unpaid, Apple/Google Wallet buttons. After check-in "You're checked in! 11:40 p.m." - the page does not poll; no table/group info (curated tables only on the event page), no late-arrival guidance (SD-21, SD-14).
- Wallet passes (`wallet/apple_event_ticket.py`, `google_event_ticket*.py`): English-only labels, 12-hour clock, **UTC time** (SD-03).
- Scan: coach POSTs `/api/events/checkin/<id>/<token>/` (+/-12h window). A member scanning their own QR with the phone camera gets an empty 405 page (SD-21).
- Door admits unpaid `pending` seats by design (docstring `views_ticket.py:49-52`): copy says "to confirm" while the door does not enforce it (SD-05).

###### D3. Curated evening [R]
- Lock requires every group member checked in ("Every active group member must be checked in before lock."), so one late/unpaid member holds up a whole group; member-facing explanation: none (SD-14, SD-21).
- After lock the event page shows "Your group is final ... Your tables: Round 1: table 1, seat A ...".
- Voting lobby still offered to attended members ("Activity Voting Lobby - You'll vote on TWO categories ... Presentation Style: How will everyone introduce themselves in 90 seconds?") (SD-02).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### E. Post-event and follow-up

###### E1. Connections `/events/<id>/attendees/` [R] - `views_connections.event_attendees`
- Opens at event end, closes at end + 48h (`connection_window_hours`). "My Crush!" flow: 1 crush per event; "Your Crush Coach will call you within 48 hours".
- **DE/FR: the explanation, privacy promise, allowance line and the "My Crush!" buttons render in English** (empty/fuzzy msgstr; fuzzy ones are also *wrong*) (SD-04).
- List contains every attended member of the event, not only the people met in the member's curated group (SD-17).

###### E2. Emails
- Feedback request (`event_feedback_request.html`, `EventFeedback` timer hourly :55 09-20 UTC, first sweep after end): "How was {title}?" - fine; transparent about coach visibility.
- Recap (`event_recap.html`, `EventRecaps` hourly :25, 24-36h after end): for a member with no connection activity it says only "It was lovely to have you. The next event is around the corner" and links to Browse events - no mention that the connection window is open for ~24 more hours (SD-08).

###### E3. My Events / dashboard [R]
- Past tab for never-selected applicants shows a "Recap" button (SD-13); icon-only cancel/ticket links have aria-labels ("Cancel registration" even for an application - SD-22).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### Cross-cutting
- **Time zone**: site, email, `.ics` and Google/Outlook/Yahoo calendar links are correct; push and wallet passes are UTC (SD-03).
- **Language**: payment endpoints are language-neutral; error text follows cookie/Accept-Language rather than the page language (not separately reported).
- **Notifications**: email is the only channel for application result, invitation, waitlist promotion, payment confirmation (SD-18).
- **Untested on Postgres** (SQLite ignores `select_for_update`): SD-01 (claim lifecycle), SD-05 (hold semantics under concurrent promotion), SD-12 (capture-time remedy locking), SD-16 (notice claiming).

### 3.2 Quiz Night

**25 findings:** 0 Blocks · 18 Hurts · 7 Polish.

#### Journey map


Scope note (what this product actually is, from code): Quiz Night is an in-person, table-based evening. Guests do NOT answer on their phones.
The host reads questions, each table discusses, the host marks every table correct/wrong in the host panel, results reveal when every
table is marked. There is no join code, team naming, or nickname: a guest "joins" by being checked in at the door (coach scan,
`views_checkin.py:560-700` -> `services/quiz_rotation.py:assign_table_on_checkin`) and then opening the live page
(`/<lang>/events/<id>/quiz/`, `views_quiz.py:186 quiz_live_view`, 404 unless registration status == "attended"). Roles are gender-derived:
M -> "anchor" (stays), F -> "rotator" (moves), NB/other -> whichever pool is smaller (`quiz_rotation.py:267-279`).
Language is URL-prefix driven (`quiz-live.js:12-19`), not profile driven and not driven by `event.languages` (real quiz nights are "de/lu" or "fr" evenings, `create_september_2026_events.py:179-188`).

Persona key: P1 first-timer on a phone, anxious about dating / unsure what "verified" or "anchor/rotator" mean. P2 returning member, wants speed.
P3 edge (not checked in, waitlisted, late arrival, wrong language, slow/dropping connection, screen reader). H = host/coach (extra persona I added
because the brief scopes the host experience).

Verification legend: [R] rendered with cx_render/qn_r.py (Django test Client, local DB). [WS] driven with channels.testing.WebsocketCommunicator
against the local SQLite DB (dev runserver is WSGI; no live sockets). [C] read code/template only.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### A. Guest journey (before the event)

1. Discover — `event_list.html:95` "Discover Quiz Night" -> `/<lang>/quiz-night/` (`views_static.quiz_night_guide`, `quiz_night_guide.html`, added by #1174). [R/C]
   Trigger: browse. Language: gettext, DE/FR msgstr shipped (checked in .po, none fuzzy).
   P1: guide says "Come on your own", "No team needed", "What should I bring? A charged phone for your table assignment and the live quiz." (good).
   Friction: this guide is NOT linked from the event page the guest later registers on (see QN-15).

2. Event page — `event_detail.html:418-433` "Quiz Night Format" box; view `views_events.event_detail`. [R as qn_conf / testuser3 / anon]
   Copy: "Teams of 4 at each table (2 men + 2 women)", "Women rotate between tables after each round", "The host reads questions and scores each table",
   "Your personal score follows you when you switch tables", "After the quiz, connect with people you clicked with!".
   P1: no mention of phone, check-in -> table, roles, or the venue screen. P3 (non-binary / prefer-not-to-say): format box is silent (QN-14).

3. Register — `event_register.html`, `views_events.event_register` (direct mode; SumUp checkout if fee > 0). [R as qn_conf, paid qn_ event 30]
   Shows title/date/place/"Event Fee: €15.50" and "Confirm Registration" only. No quiz-specific content (shared flow with Speed Dating; money-rule gaps belong to the sd review).
   FR back link renders "Retour à la connexion" (QN-18).

4. Confirmation email — `emails/event_registration_confirmation.html`, `email_helpers` (user preferred language, Graph/SMTP). [C]
   "What to bring: Valid ID (for age verification) / An open mind and positive attitude / Your best self!" — generic for all event types; no phone, no check-in -> table explanation (QN-15).

5. Reminder email — `management/commands/send_event_reminders.py` (day-before sweep; optional `--hours-before` same-day mode) -> `email_helpers.send_event_reminder`
   -> `emails/event_reminder.html`. [C] Same generic "Remember to bring" block. FR msgstr "N'oublie pas d'apporter :" is informal tu (tone: see #1166).
   No push / WhatsApp / SMS touchpoint exists for Quiz Night (grep of push/whatsapp services: none).

##### B. Guest journey (at the venue)

6. Ticket + check-in — `views_ticket.py` / `event_ticket.html`: QR; after scan shows "You're checked in!" + "Table N" (`event_ticket.html:96-101`). [R/C]
   Coach scan -> `assign_table_on_checkin` + printed badge payload + `_broadcast_quiz_table_update` (`views_checkin.py:588-600, 692-694`).
   Friction: ticket shows the round-0 check-in table only (`views_ticket.py:109-122`) and has no "Join Quiz" button (QN-22).

7. Projector waiting screen — `/<lang>/quiz/<id>/display/` (`views_quiz.quiz_table_display`, `quiz_display.html`, `quiz-display.js`). [R]
   Roster of tables with first names, "⚓ anchor / ↻ rotator" labels, "N checked in", QR "Scan to join / Sign in and complete event check-in first.
   The QR code does not bypass attendance access." Language = projector URL prefix; QR encodes the same language.
   P1: finds table by first name on screen (good). Privacy: nothing tells guests their name/photo is projected (QN-21).

8. Open the live page on the phone — entry points: event page hero "Join Quiz" (`event_detail.html:470-474`, only when attended), dashboard
   `post_event_actions` (`views.py:606-617`), or projector QR. View `views_quiz.py:193-198` -> `Http404` unless attended. [R as qn_conf, qn_wait, qn_cancel, anon]
   Not checked in: "We couldn't find that page — The page you're looking for may have moved, or it never existed." Anonymous: 302 to /accounts/login/?next=… (fine). (QN-06)
   Attended but unseated: page loads with NO table info and no message (QN-11). "Join Quiz" also shows when the event has no QuizEvent (404) and after the quiz ended (QN-16).

9. WebSocket connect — `consumers.py:QuizConsumer.connect` (accepts host/staff/confirmed|attended registration) -> `quiz.state` + `quiz.my_assignment`. [WS]
   Note the mismatch: the page requires "attended", the socket accepts "confirmed" too (harmless, but the two gates disagree).

##### C. Guest journey (during the quiz) — `quiz_live.html` + `quiz-live.js` (`quizLive`)

10. Waiting — "Get ready / Waiting for the quiz to start... / The host will launch the first question soon." Table badge is a collapsed `<details>` row "T3 · 📌 Anchor · N pts".
    P1: "T3", "Anchor", "Rotator" unexplained (QN-14/QN-24).

11. Question — progress "1 / 6" + "30s" countdown, bar (aria-hidden), question text in the URL language, A/B/C/D cards that are NOT tappable (quiz night: `div`s,
    `quiz-live.js:701-703`). No instruction how the table answers, no "time's up" cue at 0s (QN-17). Screen-reader live region announces progress + question text. [C/WS]

12. Scoring + reveal — host marks tables; players get `quiz.table_scored` (no content) then `quiz.reveal_scores` when ALL tables are marked. Phone shows "+N pts!" or
    "Incorrect" (hard-coded English, `quiz-live.js:538-542`) on the "Question complete" review screen; Leaderboard `<details>` updates. [WS]
    If any table (even an empty one) is unmarked, nobody ever gets feedback (QN-07).

13. Round complete — `quiz.status{round_complete}` -> `screen="waiting"` (`quiz-live.js:506-508`): phones show "Waiting for the quiz to start..." mid-quiz (QN-03).

14. Rotate — `quiz.rotate` -> "Next round / Time to rotate! / Move to Table N!" (English JS string) with the table number re-fetched asynchronously (QN-05: English-only string, and the destination is read from a value that is refreshed asynchronously).

15. Pause / resume / reset — `quiz.status{paused|active|draft}`: phones ignore paused and draft (QN-10). Resume re-broadcasts the question with a fresh full timer (`consumers.py:1363-1365`).

16. Reconnect / language switch — exponential backoff 1,2,4,8,16,30 s ×15 (~8 min), cap 20, then a 5-second error and a permanently "Reconnecting..." label (QN-09).
    Reconnect after the reveal re-shows the question and drops the result feedback (QN-04). Switching language = full reload = same path.

17. Late arrival — checks in mid-quiz, gets a seat but no current-round schedule row: sees a table, earns 0 points that round, host not alerted (QN-02). [WS reproduced]

18. Finished — "Final results / Quiz Complete! / Thanks for playing! / Your Score N pts / Final Results / Top Players / [Back to Event]". FR CTA: "Retour à la connexion" (QN-18).

##### D. After the event

19. Event page — Join Quiz stays for 2 days (`models/events.py:1273`), label unchanged after finish (QN-16); "View Attendees & Connections" opens at the SCHEDULED end (`connections_open`),
    not when the quiz finishes; no explanation of when it opens (QN-23).
20. Dashboard — same `show_quiz` CTA (`dashboard.html:169-172`).
21. Recap / feedback emails — `send_event_recaps` / `send_event_feedback_requests`: generic; no quiz score, table rank, or certificate (QN-23). Result lives only on the live page.

##### E. Host journey (coach) — extra persona H

H1. Coach event page — `coach_event_detail.html:49-66`: "Configure Quiz", "Quiz Host Panel", "Quiz Live View". No projector link (QN-12). Host Panel link 404s for unassigned coaches (QN-20).
H2. Authoring — `/coach/events/<id>/quiz/config/` (`coach_quiz_config.html`, `views_quiz_config.py`): create quiz, tables, rounds, questions with EN/DE/FR tabs, media + a11y description,
    Readiness Check (rounds, questions, answers, tables, registrations, assignments, photos — no translation or projector check, QN-25). "Update" tables has no warning (QN-13). [R/C + rolled-back txn test]
H3. Door — check-in assigns tables; Host Panel shows "Unassigned attendees", "Consolidate Tables (after no-shows)", "Dissolve table" (top empty table only).
H4. Run of show — `quiz_coach.html` + `quizHost`: Start, Next Question (hidden until all tables scored), Score Tables grid (every table incl. empty ones), Rotate Tables, Leaderboard,
    Pause/Resume, End Quiz (2-tap), Reset (2-tap, always clears scores). Errors: "All tables must be scored before advancing.", "Only the highest-numbered table can be dissolved (table 4). Got table 3." [WS]
    Mistakes: score tap locks (QN-08), End is terminal (QN-01), ghost tables (QN-07), buttons dead when offline (QN-19).

##### F. Touchpoint inventory (what exists / what does not)
- Email: registration confirmation, reminder, (waitlist/cancel/refund shared), recap, feedback request — none quiz-specific.
- Push / WhatsApp / SMS: none for Quiz Night.
- In-app: event page format box, ticket table number, dashboard Join Quiz, live phone page, projector, host panel.
- Printed: door badge with table (`services/ticket_printer.py`, not reviewed in detail).

### 3.3 Crush Connect

**24 findings:** 1 Blocks · 19 Hurts · 4 Polish.

#### Journey map


Reviewer: CX product 3. Date of review: 2026-10-05. Repo HEAD `ec37261`. Everything below is grounded in templates, views, services, `.po` files, or pages I rendered locally (EN/DE/FR) with the Django test client (a page-render helper, wrapped by my a wrapper script) and, for two mobile checks, Chromium at 390x844. No prod data was available; where data would settle a question I write `needs data: <metric>`.

Persona keys: **P1** first-timer (28-45, phone, anxious, unsure what "verified" means). **P2** returning member (wants speed, hates re-entering things). **P3** edge case (unverified / paused / wrong language / no photo / screen reader / pool empty).

##### 0. Phases and gating matrix (what "verified" requires)

Phase flags (`azureproject/settings.py:694-706`, `crush_lu/connect_phase.py`): `CRUSH_CONNECT_CANDIDATE_OPEN` (BETA), `CRUSH_CONNECT_LAUNCHED` (LIVE), `CRUSH_EVENT_LOBBY_ENABLED` (default False), `PREMIUM_REDIRECTS_TO_BETA` (default True). Prod values are unknown to me: `needs data: prod values of the four flags`. I rendered in BETA (default for my wrapper) and LIVE.

"Verified" has three different meanings in code:

| Meaning | Rule | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Profile verified | `verification_status == "verified"` (coach call/admin, LuxID, or coach at event) | `models/profiles.py` (CrushProfile) |
| Connect identity verified | LuxID linked OR coach-authenticated event attendance | `CrushProfile.is_connect_identity_verified` (`profiles.py:1142`), `services/crush_connect.py:47-115` |
| Event Lobby member | Connect-onboarded + not paused + **LuxID** + photo consent + photo | `services/event_lobby.py:126-160` (`participant_gate`) |

Feature access by member state (BETA unless stated):

| Member state | Teaser | Onboarding | The Mix (visible, can receive/accept, chat) | Connect Week (2 cards/day x 5 days, 1 request) | Event Lobby | Coach's Pick |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| no profile / incomplete / pending review | yes | bounced to teaser | no | no | no | no |
| approved by coach call, no LuxID, never attended | yes (verification options) | bounced to teaser | no | no | no | no |
| LuxID only | redirected past teaser | yes | yes | **no** (`cycle_access_open`, `connect_phase.py:44-61`) | yes | only with Premium |
| event-verified (coach at door), no LuxID | redirected past teaser | yes | yes | yes | **no** (`GATE_NO_LUXID`) | only with Premium |
| selected waitlist tester / staff | n/a | yes | yes | yes | LuxID rule still applies | only with Premium |
| paused | hub paused card | n/a | hidden | `/week/` and inbox redirect to hub | no | no |
| excluded by coach | silently bounced to teaser | bounced | hidden | bounced | no | no |
| LIVE phase, LuxID only | n/a | yes | yes | **yes** (`connect_phase.py:44`) | yes | only with Premium |

| 40 | Paused state | `hub.html:10-37` | hub | Clear card + one button. Does not mention a pending request or the profile-level "break" that can cause it (not filed; low confidence). |
| 41 | Leave Connect | none | n/a | Emails/explainer promise "leave anytime from your dashboard/settings"; only Pause exists (CC-13). |
| 42 | Delete account | `delete_account_confirm.html`, `gdpr_data_management.html` | settings | Not Connect-specific; Connect data removal = account deletion. |

##### 8. Notification inventory (Connect)

| Event | Channels today | Language | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|
| Request received | email + push + bell | recipient's | none (model for others) |
| Request accepted ("It's mutual!") | bell only | accepter's active language (code reading) | no email/push to requester |
| Request declined / expired | none | n/a | by design silent; requester only sees on next visit |
| Review opens / closes | none | n/a | 24h window with no reminder |
| Daily cards ready | none | n/a | missed day = lost cards |
| New chat message | none (nav badge only, cached 60 s) | n/a | requires member to open app |
| Chat closing soon | none (`reminder_sent` never set) | n/a | |
| Coach pick proposed | bell only | coach's active language | paid feature, no email/push |
| Pick accepted/declined (coach) | bell to coach | member's language | n/a |
| Event Lobby mutual signal | live WebSocket banner only | n/a | member who leaves the page misses it |
| Encounter confirmed ("{name} was added to People I've Met.") | bell only (`services/event_lobby.py:1189-1230`, "no push, email, APNS, or SMS") | recipient's (uses `translation.override`, the right pattern) but the msgid is FUZZY in DE/FR so it ships in English (CC-18) | no follow-up path (CC-09) |
| Beta invites | email (3 waves) | recipient's | German quote in EN/FR copy |

### 3.4 Crush Coach and Coach Panel

**25 findings:** 1 Blocks · 13 Hurts · 11 Polish.

#### Journey map


Reviewer: CX review, 2026-10-05. Everything below is grounded in code read in the repo or pages rendered locally
(test client via a coach-login render helper, a copy of a page-render helper with one SQLite-only patch; Chromium/Playwright at 390x844 for the
door page). Seeded local users all carry the `coach_` prefix (`coach_free_pending`, `coach_sub_*`, `coach_door_*`, event 24
"coach_ Door Night"). No usage numbers exist in this repo: where a number would settle a question it is written as
"needs data: ...". Finding ids (CO-xx) refer to Appendix A.

##### 0. Framing: what "Crush Coach" is today (read this first)

The July 2026 verification pivot (shipped 2026-07-11, see `crush_lu/management/commands/expire_stale_submissions.py:3-9` and
`crush_lu/admin/verification_queues.py:3-14`) replaced the coach review/screening-call queue with two self-serve paths: LuxID, or
"scan at the event door". `complete_profile_submission` no longer creates a `ProfileSubmission`
(`views_profile.py:754-760`); only a member who already holds a `revision`/`recontact_coach` row is re-queued as
`pending` on resubmission (`views_profile.py:762-785`), which keeps the legacy screening-call copy reachable. So the member-facing "Crush Coach" is now:

1. a name-and-first-names intro in onboarding (`onboarding/coach_intro.html`),
2. the person at the door who scans the ticket QR (which auto-verifies, `views_checkin.py::_auto_verify_on_attendance`),
3. a "permanent coach" silently granted on first attendance (`signals.py:4199`), used to gate `coach_assigned` events,
4. the paid Premium coach (choose-coach, payment recovery owned by #1163/#925/#1164 — not reviewed),
5. the post-event "My Crush!" lead where the coach phones the member within 48 h (`services/crush_leads.py`),
6. Crush Connect "Coach's Pick".

Legacy coach-review/screening-call surfaces still ship (profile_submitted states, book/<token>, meet_coach, recontact/revision
emails, coach Inbox/SLA, Coach Settings availability). Their reach is unknown: **needs data: count of ProfileSubmission rows with
status pending/revision/recontact_coach or is_paused, submitted after 2026-07-11 or spared by the cleanup (paused, future booked slot).**

`/en/crush-coach/` is NOT a member explainer: it is a coach *recruitment* page ("Become a Crush Coach"). There is no public
page that explains what a member's coach does (CO-14, CO-24).

Personas: P1 first-timer (28-45, phone, anxious); P2 returning member (speed); P3 edge case (unverified/rejected/waitlisted/
payment pending/wrong language/slow connection). Staff personas: S1 volunteer coach at the door on a phone; S2 coach/admin at a desk.

---

##### A. Member-facing journey

###### A1. Discovery: `/en/crush-coach/` (`views_static.py:216`, `crush_coach.html`)
- Trigger: nav/footer "Become a Coach" (`Werde Coach` / `Devenir Coach` in DE/FR nav for every logged-in member).
- Content: hero "Become a Crush Coach", stat tiles "In Person / Verify at Events / 3 Languages / Flexible Hours", feature cards, "Who We're Looking For", commitment list, CTA with two personal phone numbers (hard-coded, untranslated).
- Language: `{% trans %}` EN/DE/FR; phone block not translatable.
- P1 note: a first-timer searching "Crush Coach" lands on a recruitment page and still does not know what *their* coach does. Page contradicts itself: "Verify at Events" vs "Fully remote (events are optional)" (CO-24).

###### A2. Onboarding step 3 "Meet the Coaches": `onboarding/coach_intro.html`, `views_profile.py::coach_intro_step`
- Trigger: after phone verification; `coach_intro_seen_at` auto-stamped on first GET. Shows up to 6 active coaches (first name, optional photo), no bios/languages.
- Copy (EN): "Our coaches host the events and verify new members face-to-face when you come along. No online review, no screening call." / "Is your point of contact if anything feels off".
- Language: fully translated; FR heading is informal ("Découvre nos coachs", `locale/fr/.../django.po:8130`) next to a formal page (CO-23).
- P1 friction: never says what is checked at the door, that a profile photo is needed, how long it takes, what happens on a mismatch, or HOW to reach the "point of contact" (a coach is assigned only after the first attendance) (CO-14). `<label>` used as a heading, 10-13 px text.

###### A3. Onboarding step 5 "Get verified": `profile_submitted.html` via `views.py::profile_submitted` (state machine)
Rendered states (EN; DE/FR spot-checked):
| State | What the member sees | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Free path, pending (`coach_free_pending`) | "Your profile is ready. Next: get verified at an event." + options card ("Come to an event" / "Verify with LuxID — Not available right now…" / Crush Connect teaser) | No date/next event, no "add a photo" nudge, LuxID teaser copy "Thanks to LuxID, join Crush Connect…" shown while LuxID is unavailable (#1166/#1163 territory, not re-reported). |
| Legacy pending + coach (`coach_sub_pending`) | "Profile Under Review … Estimated review time 12-24 hours … Prepare for your screening call … The call usually takes 5-10 minutes" | Contradicts A2 ("No online review, no screening call") (CO-16). |
| Legacy revision (`coach_sub_rev`) | "Almost There! A Few Tweaks Needed … Coach Feedback: …" + "Update Your Profile" | Coherent; still a live legacy state ("Approved / rejected / revision submissions are never touched", expire_stale_submissions.py:28-29). |
| Legacy recontact (`coach_sub_recontact`) | "Your coach is waiting to hear from you … 11 days left … Complete the call to get your profile approved" | Only channel in my render: "Email Coach" (seed coach has no phone). |
| Fallback offered (`coach_sub_fallback`) | "Book your screening call … Pick a time" -> `/book/<token>/` | Booking page empty state: "No coaches are accepting bookings right now…" (CO-17). |
| Rejected (`coach_sub_rej`, `coach_rejected_door`) | redirect to `/profile/rejected/` | CO-01, CO-03. |
- Notification touchpoints: `send_profile_submission_confirmation` (subject "Profile Submitted for Review - Crush.lu", body says "no waiting" - subject/body mismatch, CO-16).
- SQLite note: this view runs `Extract(epoch)` on a DurationField when >= 5 reviewed submissions exist; SQLite 500s, Postgres does not. Environment artifact, not reported as a finding.

###### A4. `onboarding/meet_coach.html` (`views_profile.py::meet_coach_step`)
- Trigger: none in code — orphan URL (only `capture_screenshots.py` references it). Says "You'll be invited to a short screening call / Once approved, you can register for events" (both false post-pivot). CO-16.

###### A5. Event registration -> ticket -> door
- `event_register`/event page for unverified members: "Complete your profile and verify your phone number. You'll get verified in person when you come to the event." (`event_detail.html:938-939`).
- Emails: `send_event_registration_confirmation` / `send_event_reminder` ("What to bring: Valid ID (for age verification)") — never mention the photo comparison or that a profile photo is needed (CO-14).
- Ticket `event_ticket.html`: QR "Show this QR code to the coach at the event entrance"; payment-due strip exists member-side (`event_ticket.html:17-34`) but the coach-side door roster has no counterpart (CO-04). QR cached offline by the service worker (good).
- At the door (member view): scan -> `event_checkin_api` -> `_auto_verify_on_attendance` -> `_run_post_verification_side_effects` -> `notify_profile_approved` email ("Welcome to Crush.lu - Your Profile is Approved!", EN/DE/FR rendered). If the member has NO photo: checked in but not verified, nothing tells them (CO-02). If the coach taps "Photo mismatch" later: `coach_reject_verification` -> `notify_profile_rejected` ("Your verification was rejected because the person at the event did not match your profile photos.") (CO-05, CO-01).
- First attendance silently grants a permanent coach (`signals.py:4199`); no email/push/dashboard card (CO-07).

###### A6. After verification: dashboard
- Verified strip + products table. "Your Crush Coach" card only renders for `latest_submission.coach` (`views.py:463`, `dashboard.html:322`) — a door-verified member never sees one; products table says "✕ No personal coach" / "✕ No coach, no weekly pick" (`partials/_products.html:48,86`). Contact is `mailto:{{coach.user.email}}` ("Contact Coach", `dashboard.html:353`). No in-app member<->coach messaging exists; the only channels are email (card), wa.me/tel (legacy states only).
- CTA "Go Premium — first month free" -> `/premium/coaches/` -> silent 302 to the Crush Connect marketing page (CO-12).

###### A7. Post-event: connections and "My Crush!"
- `event_attendees.html:194,245` / `request_connection.html:49,67-68`: "Your crush stays completely private — they are never notified" and "Your Crush Coach will call you within 48 hours". The recipient's own coach is told to "Reach out to them" (`coach_crush_outreach_task.html:22`) (CO-15). 48 h promise rests on a push-only 24 h coach reminder (CO-09).
- Language: coach-side strings for the whole co-coach outreach task and the reminder push have EMPTY DE/FR msgstr (CO-20).

###### A8. Premium coach pick
- `premium/choose_coach.html` / `views_premium.py` — payment recovery is #1163/#925/#1164 (not re-reported). Reviewed only the entry: `PREMIUM_REDIRECTS_TO_BETA` defaults True (`settings.py:706`), so most members never reach the picker (CO-12).

###### A9. Failure / edge touchpoints
- Door-rejected member: dashboard, event page and profile page disagree and loop (CO-01).
- Stale booking link -> bare 404 (CO-17).
- Wrong language: DE/FR screens verified for coach-intro, profile_submitted, emails; DE/FR gaps listed in CO-20/22/23.

---

##### B. Coach Panel (staff-facing)

###### B1. Coach dashboard `/en/coach/dashboard/` (`views_coach.py::coach_dashboard`, `coach_dashboard.html`)
- Stats: "Approved Profiles / Awaiting Verification / Your Total Reviews / Connections Pending"; nav groups Review / People / Activities; "Pending reviews — by SLA" (legacy).
- No link to tonight's check-in; Events -> event -> "Check-In Scanner" = 3 taps (CO-13). "Your Total Reviews" counts legacy submissions.

###### B2. Inbox `/coach/queue/` (`coach_action_queue.html`)
- "Profile reviews, screening calls, and connections needing your action — sorted by urgency." Tabs Profiles/Calls/Connections/Crush calls/Outreach. Legacy-heavy, crush leads and outreach appear here. SLA chips ("On track / Escalated / Breached").

###### B3. Unverified profiles `/coach/unverified/` (`coach_unverified_profiles.html`)
- Good: search, signal filter ("Attended but unverified", "No photo", "Booked on an event"), per-row links to the event door. Gaps: "No coach owns it" chip appears on every row now (CO-21); "A door scan skips profiles without a photo" is only a `title` tooltip (touch users never see it).

###### B4. Review queue `/coach/review/<id>/` (`coach_review_profile.html`) — legacy submission review (not deep-reviewed, see Section 7.4)

###### B5. Roster `/coach/members/` (`coach_members.html`)
- "My Members ... No approved members yet — Members will appear here once you approve their profiles." Keyed on approved ProfileSubmissions (`views_coach.py:1322-1331`) -> empty for door-verified/assigned members (CO-08).
- Event roster (`coach_event_detail`, counts) is being changed by #1176: not reviewed.

###### B6. Event check-in `/coach/events/<id>/checkin/` (`coach_event_checkin.html`, `js/alpine/coach.js::coachCheckin`, `views_checkin.py`)
Verified by Chromium at 390x844 with 8 seeded attendees:
- Strengths (keep): counters (Checked In / Not arrived / Expected), live gender split, search by name/legal name/email, "Show only who has not arrived", 44 px-high primary buttons, undo (15 min window, restores prior status incl. waitlist/pending, revokes door-granted coach and auto-verification), waitlist promote section, vendored QR library, WebSocket live sync across coaches, thermal-printer hook.
- Friction: no payment marker on pending seats (CO-04); no reason shown for "Unverified" (no photo / rejected / incomplete) (CO-06); Verify (green) sits 4 px from Check In (CO-06); "Photo mismatch" exists only in a 5 s toast after a live check-in (CO-05); offline/slow = blocking `alert("Network error")`, ambiguous "Offline"/"Network error or invalid QR code." (CO-18); scan-result strings are raw English f-strings, not actionable (CO-19); 11/45 template strings absent from DE/FR catalogue incl. "Photo mismatch" + its reject confirmation (CO-20).
- Language handling: server JSON on undo/verify/reject uses `_()`; the scan path does not.

###### B7. Curated applications / groups (`components/coach_curated_groups.html`)
- Read-only panel with "Generation N is a draft / provisional / locked / degraded", "What the projector would do with the pool right now", "Open the coach panel ->" (link goes to the admin generator). Jargon for volunteer coaches (CO-25). Could not populate applicants in the time available (see skipped).

###### B8. Connections / My Crush! (`coach_connections.html`, `coach_connection_review.html`, `coach_crush_outreach_task.html`)
- Empty state clear ("No connections need review right now."). 48 h SLA copy exists staff-side ("Only 'Call completed' closes the 48h SLA") but is untranslated DE/FR (CO-20). Reminder = push only (CO-09).

###### B9. Coach notifications/push (`coach_notifications.py`, `api_coach_push.py`, `partials/edit_account_notifications.html:307-438`)
- Toggles live in the *member* Account -> Notifications page, not in Coach Settings. Labels: "New Profile Submissions", "Screening Reminders — Reminders about pending screening calls", "User Revisions", "System Alerts". The "Screening Reminders" flag also gates "My Crush! call still open" (CO-09). No coach push exists for "event starts soon / check-in open".

###### B10. Coach Settings `/coach/settings/` (`coach_settings.html`)
- Entirely about legacy screening scheduling ("How do you prefer to schedule screening calls?", availability windows, "Max concurrent reviews") (CO-21).

###### B11. Campaigns console `/crush-admin/campaigns/` (`admin/campaign_dashboard.py`, `templates/admin/crush_lu/campaign_*.html`)
- 4-step composer (Audience -> Channels & content -> Preview & estimate -> Schedule). Good: per-channel consent noted, estimate/preview steps, WhatsApp template picker with language variants.
- Gaps: audience "Users awaiting coach approval" resolves via legacy queue (CO-10); "Send now" copy promises "within ~5 minutes" regardless of `CAMPAIGN_DISPATCH_ENABLED` (default False) and "Send at (UTC)" for Luxembourg operators (CO-11). Admin pages are forced to English (AGENTS.md), so fuzzy DE/FR there is not a finding.

---

##### C. Touchpoint inventory (member-facing messages)

| Touchpoint | Trigger | Function / template | Language handling |
|---|---|---|---|
| Profile submission confirmation email | profile completed | `email_helpers.send_profile_submission_confirmation`, `emails/profile_submission_confirmation.html` | user's preferred language; subject says "for Review" (CO-16) |
| "Profile approved" email + in-app | door auto-verify / manual Verify / LuxID / legacy approval | `notification_service.notify_profile_approved` -> `send_profile_approved_notification` | user language; FR mixes vous/tu (CO-22) |
| "Profile needs updates" email | legacy revision verdict | `send_profile_revision_request` | DE "Trainer"/typos, FR "Profil soumis pour examen !" (CO-22) |
| "Profile not approved" email + bell | legacy rejection or door "Photo mismatch" | `send_profile_rejected_notification`, `notify_profile_rejected` | user language; promises "create a new profile" (CO-03) |
| "Your coach needs to speak with you" email | legacy recontact | `send_profile_recontact_notification` (wa.me + tel + mailto) | user language |
| Screening confirmed / fallback offered / pre-screening invite emails | legacy flow | `emails/screening_*.html`, `pre_screening_notifications.py` | not reviewed in depth (legacy) |
| Event registration / reminder emails | register / T-1 day | `send_event_registration_confirmation`, `send_event_reminder` | user language; "Valid ID" bullet (CO-14) |
| Coach push: new submission, screening reminder, user revision, system alert, My Crush! reminder | various | `coach_notifications.py` | coach language via `user_language_context`; My Crush! reminder msgstr EMPTY DE/FR (CO-09/CO-20) |
| WhatsApp | campaign dispatch (`services/campaigns.py`); coach wa.me deep links in legacy states | Meta template per language (EN/DE/FR) | recipient language; missing variants fail per recipient |
| Click redirect | campaign links `/c/<token>/` | `views_campaign_click.py` | language-neutral |

### 3.5 Cross-cutting member journey

**24 findings:** 0 Blocks · 14 Hurts · 10 Polish.

#### Journey map


Product 5 (`xc`): signup/login, onboarding wizard, "verified" explanations, notifications and emails, cancellations and Crush Credit, account/privacy, mobile/PWA.
Reviewed 2026-10-05 against the local repo only (no prod data). Findings are in Appendix A (ids `XC-01`..`XC-24`); Section 7.5 lists what I did not cover.

Personas: **P1** first-timer (28-45, phone, anxious, unsure what "verified" means) / **P2** returning member (speed, hates re-entering) / **P3** edge case (unverified, waitlisted, cancelled, payment failed, wrong language, slow connection, screen reader).

How each step was checked: `R` = rendered with the Django test client as an `xc_*` user (EN/DE/FR where noted), `E` = email rendered through the real `email_helpers` function with a stubbed sender (real templates, real context), `C` = read code/template only, `X` = executed code locally (signup/confirm/login/reset flows, `delete_crushlu_profile_only`).

Local users I created (all `xc_*`; the signup-flow users got auto-generated usernames such as `elodie`, which I renamed to the `xc_` prefix afterwards, their emails were always `xc_*@example.com`): `xc_noprofile`, `xc_welcome`, `xc_phone`, `xc_coach`, `xc_incomplete`, `xc_submitted_de` (pending), `xc_approved_fr` (verified; has registrations on events 9/12/14/22), `xc_rejected`, `xc_unverified_email`, `xc_deltest` (profile deleted by the deletion experiment), plus signup-flow users `xc_flow_fr`, `xc_langflow`, `xc_mkt`, `xc_greet_en/de/fr`. Registrations and credit rows exist only for these users. `testuser1` received a password-reset email through a locmem backend in a script (no row changes besides the reset key).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

##### 1. Signup, login, verification (steps A1-A10)

| # | Screen / message | Template / code | Trigger | Language handling | Check |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | Login/Signup tabs ("Create your Crush.lu account", LuxID "Recommended" button, Google/Facebook/Microsoft/Apple only if configured, "≈ 15 min to set up") | `crush_lu/auth.html`, `views_account.signup`, `urls.UnifiedAuthView` | `/xx/signup/`, `/xx/login/`; signup rate-limited 5/h per IP (inline error, 429) | Path prefix `/xx/`; LuxID button text only when the provider is configured (`has_luxid`) | R EN/DE/FR (social buttons not rendered locally: no SocialApps) |
| A2 | Signup validation: duplicate email ("An account with this email already exists... [Sign in with this email]"), weak password, missing consent ("You must consent to use Crush.lu services") | `forms.CrushSignupForm` | POST signup | translated | X |
| A3 | Flash "Account created! Check your email and complete your profile." then **verification-sent page** ("Check Your Inbox", masked address, "Wrong address? Sign up again", resend with 3-min cooldown, "Use a different address") | `account/verification_sent_crush.html`, `views_account.resend_verification_email` (3/h per IP) | `complete_signup` under `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_VERIFICATION="mandatory"` | **Language-neutral URL** `/accounts/confirm-email/` -> browser language, not the signup path (XC-06) | R + X |
| A4 | **Verification email** (allauth `email_confirmation_signup_message`): subject "Welcome to Crush.lu — please confirm your email", greeting = generated username (XC-22), link valid 3 days (not stated, XC-09) | `account/email/*` | allauth on signup, resend, or login while unverified | Language of the requesting browser/path at send time | E/X EN/DE/FR |
| A5 | Confirm page ("Confirm Your Email" button) then redirect to `/<lang>/login/` with prefilled email + **two** success banners (XC-22) | `account/email_confirm_crush.html`, `signals.stash_confirmed_email_for_login_prefill`, `adapters.get_email_verification_redirect_url` | click link, POST | Active language (Accept-Language) -> `/en/login/` even after a `/fr/` signup (XC-06) | R + X |
| A6 | Login (email + password) -> `/dashboard/` (profile auto-created at first login) | `adapters._get_crush_redirect_url`, `signals.create_crush_profile_on_login` | email/password login | `preferred_language` set from the login request's language; the "sync" signal is a no-op (XC-06) | X |
| A7 | Login before verifying -> "Check Your Inbox" page with a generic sentence and no reason (XC-09) | same verification-sent template | allauth blocks unverified login | neutral URL | X |
| A8 | Expired/invalid confirm link -> "Link Expired / Request a New Link" -> A3 page claiming "We've sent a verification link" (XC-09) | `email_confirm_crush.html` | click old link | neutral | R |
| A9 | Password reset: form -> "Check your email" (DE "Auf Updates prüfen", FR "Vérifier les mises à jour", XC-16) -> mail ("This link will expire in a few hours" vs real 72 h, XC-09) -> new-password page -> done | `account/password_reset*.html`, `account/email/password_reset_key_message.*` | `/accounts/password/reset/` (language-neutral) | Browser language, not saved preference (XC-06) | R + E EN/FR |
| A10 | Social signup/login (LuxID, Google, Facebook, Microsoft, Apple): `socialaccount/signup_crush.html` when no email, `oauth_landing`, welcome email (social only, XC-17), consent confirmation `consent_confirm.html` for legacy accounts | `signals.*create_crush_profile_from_*`, `views_account.oauth_complete` | OAuth callback | `oauth_signup_request` language | C only (needs provider credentials) |

Persona notes
- P1: the waiting page is the first moment of doubt - wrong-language page, "sent" when nothing was sent, no deadline, no support contact, masked address hides typos (XC-06, XC-09). First greeting is a lower-cased username (XC-22).
- P2: after the email click the member retypes the password they just chose (deliberate: `ACCOUNT_LOGIN_ON_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION` off) and lands on the dashboard, not the journey (XC-15).
- P3: rate limits are explained inline and with `Retry-After` (good); resend cooldown is shown as a countdown (good); duplicate-email path has a "Sign in with this email" shortcut (good).

##### 2. Onboarding wizard (steps B1-B8)

| # | Screen | Template / view | Trigger / gate | Time shown | Check |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | `/welcome/` - intent probe (4 options, saved via HTMX), "The road ahead", "What makes us different" | `welcome.html`, `views_profile.welcome_view`, `welcome_intent_api` | stamps `welcome_seen_at` | "≈ 1 min", road ahead "≈ 12 min" | R EN |
| B2 | `/onboarding/phone/` - LuxID card (Recommended), SMS code, WhatsApp code (if configured), OTP modal with "via WhatsApp / Verify by SMS instead", WhatsApp support link | `onboarding/phone.html`, `includes/phone_otp_modal.html`, `views_phone_verification` | needs `welcome_seen_at` | "≈ 1 min" | R EN (modal markup only; no live OTP) |
| B3 | `/onboarding/coach-intro/` - "Real coaches, real events", "No online review, no screening call" | `onboarding/coach_intro.html` | needs `phone_verified`; GET stamps `coach_intro_seen_at` | "≈ 2 min" | R EN |
| B4 | `/create-profile/` 4 sub-steps: **Basic info** (DOB, gender, canton/region), **Event identity** (vibe, interests <=8, "ask me about" <=3, event vibe; all optional), **Photos** (1-3 optional) + privacy toggles + event languages (required), **Review & Submit** with "What Happens Next?" | `create_profile.html`, `views_profile.save_profile_step1/2/3`, `complete_profile_submission`, drafts in `profile.draft_data` | journey guard redirects GET to `/onboarding/` if steps 1-3 incomplete | "≈ 8 min" | R EN (xc_incomplete); read JS gates |
| B5 | "Profile complete!" flash then `/profile-submitted/` ("Get verified": event or LuxID; Premium upsell) | `profile_submitted.html` (**#1163 owns**) | sets `verification_status='pending'`; **no ProfileSubmission, no email** | - | R EN |
| B6 | Dashboard per state: no profile -> redirect to create; incomplete "Finish your profile / Step 1 of 4"; pending "Get verified to join"/"Verifiziere dich, um beizutreten"; verified "You're verified"; rejected "Profile not approved" | `dashboard.html`, `partials/_verification_journey.html` (**#1163**) | - | - | R EN/DE/FR |
| B7 | Rejected: `/profile/rejected/` | `profile_rejected.html` | `verification_status='rejected'` | - | R EN (XC-01) |
| B8 | Reminder emails if the profile stays incomplete: 24 h (created 24-72 h ago), 72 h (72-120 h), final (7-9 d), each at most once, gated by "Profile Updates" | `emails/profile_incomplete_*.html`, `management/commands/send_profile_reminders.py` | timer sweep | member language | E EN/DE/FR |

Persona notes
- P1: phone number is asked **before** any product value is shown (B2) and the page never says what the number is used for beyond "keeps Crush.lu real and spam-free"; the wizard's photo step never says who sees photos (XC-13); "Free via POST" / "LuxID" are undefined (XC-23).
- P2: autosave and resume exist ("Welcome back — we restored the answers you hadn't finished saving"), the stepper dots are clickable for completed steps, and `/onboarding/` is a smart-resume entry - good. Asked twice: nothing identical, but phone shown again read-only in step 1 (fine) and, later, speed-dating preferences per event.
- P3: step-1 **Continue** is disabled when no gender is chosen with no inline reason (`core.js:3704` `cannotContinueStep1 = !phoneVerified || gender === ""`); only the phone case has an amber explanation (not filed separately, see Section 7.5).
- Time promises conflict: 15 min (signup) / 12 min (welcome) / 5-10 min (welcome email) / 5 min (reminders) (XC-21).
- "Profile submitted" waiting state: there is **no review queue** any more (`complete_profile_submission` comments); the member is told to come to an event or use LuxID. "How long does review take" is therefore moot, but the nav still says "Not Submitted" (XC-07) and an "Under Review" label exists for legacy rows.

##### 3. What "verified" means - every place it is explained

| Surface | Wording (EN) | Note |
|---|---|---|
| Signup tab strip | "Get verified at your first event" / "Get verified instantly with LuxID, or at an event" | gated on LuxID being configured |
| Welcome step | "Verified, real people - A coach verifies you in person at an event — or use LuxID." | LuxID undefined |
| Phone step | "One tap verifies your number and your identity — your profile goes live the moment you submit it." | true via lazy fix-up in `profile_submitted` (checked in `signals.auto_approve_profile_on_luxid_connect`) |
| Coach intro | "Verifies you face-to-face when you arrive — that's all it takes" | no mention of ID |
| Wizard review | "Come to an event and a coach verifies you in person, or connect your LuxID in seconds — no waiting." | |
| Event confirmation/reminder emails | "Valid ID (for age verification)" | only place ID is named (XC-12) |
| Dashboard | pending: "Get verified to join" + "Identität verifizieren"; verified: "Vous êtes vérifié(e)" | `_verification_*` is #1163 |
| Door push/bell/email | "Your profile is approved" | vocabulary split (XC-14) |
| Nav dropdown / edit profile badge | "Not Submitted" for pending (XC-07), "Approved ✓" / "Profile Approved" for verified | |
| What it unlocks | post-event connections and Crush Connect (`EventRegistration.can_make_connections`), **not** event registration (pending members can register) | |
