| `existing_user_invitation.html`, `external_guest_invitation.html`, `invitation_approved.html`, `invitation_rejected.html` | `email_notifications.py` (private invitations) | none | override | not reviewed |
| `journey_gift_notification.html` | `views_journey_gift.py:152` | none | override | not reviewed |
| `pre_screening_invite.(html,txt)`, `screening_confirmed.(html,txt)`, `screening_fallback_offered.(html,txt)` | `pre_screening_notifications.py`, `views_booking.py:321`, `tasks.py:298` (SLA) | none | override | legacy pre-event screening; likely dormant after the verification pivot - not rendered |
| `base_email.html` | layout: header, footer with Unsubscribe + "Manage email preferences" (shown only when an unsubscribe URL exists; absent on transactional mails such as payment-pending, waitlist, cancellation) | - | - | E |
| `account/email/email_confirmation(_signup)_message.(txt,html)`, `*_subject.txt`, `password_reset_key_message.(txt,html)` | allauth | n/a | **browser language at send time** | E EN/DE/FR; greeting username (XC-22); "a few hours" (XC-09) |
| `crush_lu/email/weekly_kpis.html` | `send_weekly_kpis` | staff | - | not member-facing |

Gating table derived by scanning `email_helpers.py`: only the following call `can_send_email`: profile submission/approved/reminders (`profile_updates`), registration confirmation/reminder/recap/feedback (`event_reminders`), connection mails (`new_connections`), messages (`new_messages`), credit expiry, beta invite. Payment-pending, waitlist, cancellation, organiser-cancel, refund, curated, receipts, welcome, rejected/revision/recontact never check preferences (XC-05).

##### 5. Push, WhatsApp and bell inventory

| Event | Email | Web/iOS/Android push | Bell row | WhatsApp |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Door verification (profile approved) | yes | yes (`profile_updates` switch) | yes | no |
| Door/coach rejection, revision, recontact | yes | yes | yes | no |
| Event reminder (day-before, optional 2 h) | yes | yes (`event_reminders`) | yes | no (although the account toggle promises it, XC-04) |
| New connection request / accepted / mutual match | yes | yes | yes | no |
| New chat message | yes | yes | yes | no (promised by the toggle, XC-04) |
| Connect Week request | yes | yes | yes | yes, optional utility template (`WHATSAPP_CONNECT_REQUEST_TEMPLATE`) |
| Registration confirmation / payment pending / waitlist added / **waitlist promotion** / cancellation / credit issued | yes (differing gates) | **no** | no | no |
| Credit expiry | yes (once per credit) | no | no | no |
| Phone verification code | - | - | - | yes (OTP, WhatsApp or SMS; support link in modal) |
| Admin campaigns (`/crush-admin/campaigns/`) | newsletter leg | push to every enabled subscription (`services/campaigns.py:455`) | - | to `whatsapp_opt_in` members (`services/campaigns.py:331`) (XC-04) |

Duplication: by design `NotificationService.notify` sends push AND email AND bell for the same event ("push success does not suppress email"); code comments in `views_connections.py:1323` say "push first, email fallback" (XC-18). The scheduled reminder has an email-only retry path so a failed email does not re-push (good).
Unsubscribe/preference clarity: footer links exist on preference-gated mails; the settings page offers Profile/Event/Connections/Messages/Marketing + Pause-all + WhatsApp + per-device push; clarity gaps are XC-03/04/05.
Push prompt: bottom sheet "Never Miss an Event! ... instant alerts about new events, connections, and messages" shown 5 s after load, only in a standalone PWA, only after a first confirmed booking; "Maybe Later" snoozes 7 days, X dismisses permanently (`_push_activation_prompt.html`). Android settings link is #1147.

##### 9. Finding index (ranked)

Blocks: XC-01.
Hurts: XC-02 deletion forfeits credit/paid seats; XC-03 marketing/newsletter consent mismatch; XC-04 WhatsApp/push consent scope; XC-05 transactional gating vs toggles; XC-06 signup language lost; XC-07 nav "Not Submitted"; XC-08 credit balance/expiry invisible; XC-09 verification/reset link claims and expiry; XC-10 waitlist email vs paid promotion; XC-11 reminder to unpaid seat; XC-12 ID disclosed late; XC-13 photo visibility unexplained; XC-14 approved vs verified; XC-15 first login lands on dashboard; XC-16 reset-flow translations; XC-17 stale "bio" and no welcome mail for password signups.
Polish: XC-18 chat fan-out; XC-19 FR tu in emails (21 strings); XC-20 DE typos/untranslated/fuzzy; XC-21 set-up time conflicts; XC-22 username greeting + double banner; XC-23 POST/LuxID jargon; XC-24 iOS Safari-only guide + offline contradiction.

Untested on Postgres (SQLite ignores `select_for_update`): XC-02, XC-08, XC-10 (promotion/credit/deletion lock ordering). Everything else is lock-independent.

## 4. Proposals: quick wins, medium improvements, bold ideas

Ranked by impact on registration completion, no-shows, repeat attendance and trust. Success metrics are all `needs data`. `[P4-n]` marks items shipped, in part or whole, as a draft PR (Section 5).


Repo HEAD `ec37261`, 2026-10-05. Source: 123 findings, 95 Blocks/Hurts challenged by 10 verifiers (Appendix A). Nothing here was implemented; no repo file was edited.

#### How to read this

* **Ranking lens** (in this order): registration completion, no-shows, repeat attendance, trust. Every item says in one line why it sits where it does.
* **Quick win (QW)** = copy/template text only, derived from a finding whose post-verifier `copy_only_fix` is true. **Medium (M)** = needs view/JS/notification/ops work or an owner decision. **Bold (B)** = a larger idea worth a spec.
* **BEFORE/AFTER** blocks give shipped copy and the proposed copy. DE is informal *du*, FR is formal *vous*. Where a verifier said the reviewer's AFTER was unsafe, I rewrote it and say so ("verifier fix").
* **`[P4-n]`** marks a quick win that is also a Phase 4 candidate bundle in the fix PR descriptions (Section 5).
* **Metrics are always "needs data: ..."**: this repo has no usage data and I did not invent any.
* **Open PR dependencies** (live file lists read through the GitHub API):
  * **#1163** Premium recovery case touches 33 files, including `dashboard.html`, `membership.html`, `partials/_products.html`, `_verification_journey.html`, `_verification_options.html`, `premium/choose_coach.html`, `profile_submitted.html`, `views.py`, `email_helpers.py`, **`views_payments.py`, `views_premium.py`** (the brief's list omitted these two) and both `.po` files near L44049 (de) / L44169 (fr).
  * **#1175** curated "No payment now" touches `event_register.html`, both `.po` files at EOF, `tests/test_curated_registration.py`.
  * **#1176** coach roster counts touches `views_coach.py` and `tests/test_coach_event_detail.py`.
  * "Sequence after #1163" below means: same file, rebase risk, do not run in parallel.
* **Untested on Postgres** (SQLite ignores `select_for_update`): SD-01, SD-05, SD-12, SD-16, QN-02, QN-07, QN-08, CC-03, CC-07, XC-02, XC-08, XC-10. Their behaviour was verified, not their lock ordering.
* **Systemic finding made during synthesis (S-01).** A regex scan of member-facing templates and top-level Python shows strings used by shipped code that have **no `.po` entry at all** in either language, so DE/FR members read English. Reproduced: `/de/events/26/` shows the heading "Your application is in!" in English. Examples: "Application Received!", "Your application is in!", the post-cancel credit flash, "Unable to initiate payment at the moment...", the Connect Week card button "Answer", the footer link "Cookie Settings" (`base.html:1341`, on every page), and all of `crush_credit_expiry_reminder.html`. This is the root cause behind several findings; see Cross-cutting M1.

---

### 1. Speed Dating

#### Quick wins

##### SD-QW1. The waitlist tells the truth: no pay button, a "you pay nothing" line, an accurate promotion email  `[P4-1 + P4-3]`
* **User problem.** SD-06: right after joining a full paid event the success screen offers "Pay with Card (15.50 EUR)" (`_event_registration_success.html:88`, condition only excludes `applied`); the click returns 400 "This registration cannot be paid for in its current state." My Events shows a ticket icon for waitlist cards that 404s (`my_events.html:104`). XC-10: the waitlist email promises "automatically moved to confirmed" (`emails/event_waitlist.html:45`) but on a paid event promotion lands on `pending` and the member gets "Payment required to confirm" (`views_events.py:90-109`, `2418-2427`).
* **Design.** Success screen: hide both pay buttons for `waitlist`, add one paid-event sentence. My Events: ticket icon only for confirmed/pending/attended. Email: paid events get a "we hold the spot and email you; you then complete the payment" bullet, free events keep the old one. No payment window is named because none exists in code (verifier: do not invent one).
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.**
  * Success screen, EN: `You're on the Waitlist! #1 / The event is currently full, but we'll notify you if a spot opens up.` + button `Pay with Card (15.50 EUR)` -> same text, no button, plus `You pay nothing unless a place opens up for you.`
  * DE: `...wir benachrichtigen dich, wennein Platz frei wird.` -> `Das Event ist derzeit ausgebucht, aber wir benachrichtigen dich, wenn ein Platz frei wird. Du zahlst nichts, solange kein Platz für dich frei wird.`
  * FR: `...mais on te préviendra si une place se libère.` -> `L'événement est complet pour le moment, mais nous vous préviendrons si une place se libère. Vous ne payez rien tant qu'une place ne se libère pas pour vous.`
  * Email bullet (paid events), EN: `If someone cancels, you'll be automatically moved to confirmed` -> `If someone cancels, we hold the spot for you and email you straight away. You then complete the payment (15.50 EUR) to confirm it.`
  * DE: `Wenn jemand storniert, wirst du automatisch in den bestätigten Status verschoben` -> `Wenn jemand storniert, halten wir den Platz für dich frei und schreiben dir sofort. Du schließt dann die Zahlung (15,50 EUR) ab, um den Platz zu bestätigen.`
  * FR: `Si quelqu'un annule, vous serez automatiquement confirmé(e)` -> `Si quelqu'un annule, nous vous réservons la place et vous écrivons immédiatement. Vous réglez alors 15,50 EUR pour la confirmer.`
* **Files.** `_event_registration_success.html`, `my_events.html`, `emails/event_waitlist.html`, both `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Display condition only; the server allow-list for payment is unchanged.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of waitlist joiners who tap a pay button (400s on `/payments/sumup/create-event-checkout/` for waitlist registrations); share of promoted waitlist members on paid events who pay, and time from promotion to payment (XC-10).
* **Dependencies.** None. `#1175` edits `event_register.html` only (not these files).
* **Why here.** First: it removes a payment button that is designed to fail at the exact moment of waitlisting.

##### SD-QW2. "My Crush!" in German and French  `[P4-4]`
* **User problem.** SD-04: on `/de/` and `/fr/events/<id>/attendees/` the headings are translated but the body is English: "My Crush! - How It Works", "Who caught your eye? Declare your crush - it's completely private...", every "My Crush!" button (`event_attendees.html:190-240`). Cause: 13 EMPTY and 17 FUZZY entries per language (30 in all) across the post-event flow (`event_attendees`, `request_connection`, `connection_detail`, flashes in `views_connections.py`). The fuzzy ones are wrong guesses ("One crush per event" -> DE "Ein Match pro Woche", "Crush declared for this event" -> "Quiz für dieses Event erstellt.") that would ship nonsense if someone just removed the flag.
* **Design.** Translate the English exactly as shipped, one FR term for the whole flow (`coup de cœur`, as already shipped for "Déclarer mon coup de cœur !"), keep the coach-introduction caveat. Do not soften "never be notified" in DE/FR only: CO-15 (owner decision) may reword the English.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER** (EN unchanged).
  * DE: `My Crush! - How It Works` (English shown) -> `Mein Crush! – So funktioniert's`. `Who caught your eye? Declare your crush...` (English) -> `Wer ist dir aufgefallen? Erkläre deinen Crush – das bleibt völlig privat, und die Person wird nie benachrichtigt. Dein Crush Coach ruft dich innerhalb von 48 Stunden an, hört deine Geschichte und arrangiert die Vorstellung persönlich.`
  * FR: `Qui a attiré votre regard ? Déclarez votre coup de cœur — cela reste totalement privé et la personne n'est jamais prévenue. Votre Crush Coach vous appellera sous 48 heures pour écouter votre histoire et organiser personnellement la présentation.`; heading `Mon coup de cœur — comment ça marche`; counter `Un coup de cœur par événement`.
* **Files.** Both `.po` only (30 entries per language; they ship inside Phase 4 bundle P4-4 together with the Connect strings, full list in the fix PR descriptions (Section 5)).
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Translation faithful to the English.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of attendees whose `preferred_language` is DE or FR; My Crush! declarations per event by language.
* **Dependencies.** None (`.po` hunks are away from #1163 and #1175). Related: #1154 (the 17 fuzzy ones are inside its count; the 13 EMPTY ones are not), CO-15 follow-up.
* **Why here.** Second: the 48-hour window after an event is where repeat attendance is decided, and a DE/FR reader meets English at that moment.

##### SD-QW3. A recap email that keeps the connection window open  `[P4-3]`
* **User problem.** SD-08: the recap (24-36 h after the event, 12-24 h before the window closes) mentions the window only when there is a mutual match or incoming request. Everyone else reads "It was lovely to have you. The next event is around the corner - keep an eye on your inbox." (`emails/event_recap.html:37-49`), an unsupported claim, and no link to the attendees page.
* **Design.** In the no-activity branch only, while `event.connection_window_active`: ask "Someone catch your eye?", print the real deadline (`event.connection_window_deadline`), button to the attendees page, keep "Browse upcoming events" as a secondary link.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.**
  * EN: `It was lovely to have you. The next event is around the corner — keep an eye on your inbox.` + button `Browse upcoming events` -> `It was lovely to have you.` / `Someone catch your eye? You can still tell us until {deadline} — it stays private and your coach will call you.` + button `Choose who caught your eye`.
  * DE: `Es war schön, dich dabeizuhaben. Das nächste Event steht schon bald an — behalte dein Postfach im Auge.` -> `Es war schön, dich dabeizuhaben.` / `Jemand aufgefallen? Du kannst es uns noch bis {deadline} sagen – das bleibt privat und dein Coach ruft dich an.` + `Wer ist dir aufgefallen?`
  * FR: `C'était un plaisir de vous avoir parmi nous. Le prochain événement approche — gardez un œil sur votre boîte de réception.` -> `C'était un plaisir de vous avoir parmi nous.` / `Quelqu'un vous a plu ? Vous pouvez encore nous le dire jusqu'au {deadline} : cela reste privé et votre coach vous appellera.` + `Dire qui vous a plu`.
* **Files.** `emails/event_recap.html`, both `.po`. `email_helpers.py` stays untouched (#1163 file; every variable is already in the context).
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Verifier fix: print the actual deadline, not "about 24 hours"; "around the corner" is unsupported in all three languages.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of attendees with zero connection activity at the 24 h mark; click-through of the recap mail to the attendees page; My Crush! declarations after the recap.
* **Dependencies.** None. The "your coach will call you" promise rests on a fragile reminder (CO-09, see Coach M3).
* **Why here.** Third: it is the only outbound reminder of the window, aimed at the members with most to gain.

#### Medium

##### SD-M1. Make a stuck checkout recoverable (SD-01, Blocks)
* **User problem.** `create_sumup_event_checkout` answers 409 "A payment checkout is already being prepared. Please wait and try again." whenever an `EventCheckoutCreationClaim` exists (`views_payments.py:335-348`); request traffic never retires a claim and `cleanup_event_checkout_claims --apply` has no timer (only a comment in `admin/payments.py:83`). Verifier: a claim is deliberately kept for any ambiguous SumUp failure (transport error, timeout, 5xx, missing checkout id; `views_payments.py:469-489`), so the first attempt shows "Unable to initiate payment at the moment" and every retry then 409s. A claim 3 days old still 409s. The credit-payment button hits the same 409.
* **Design.** (1) Ops, propose only: run `cleanup_event_checkout_claims --apply --minutes 10 --limit 20` on the existing `hybrid-maintenance` timer and alert when an active claim is older than N minutes. (2) Copy (verifier fix, because "still preparing" can be false for a stale claim): 409 -> EN `We couldn't open your payment page yet. If it still doesn't open after a few minutes, email support@crush.lu with the event name and we'll sort it out.` DE `Wir konnten deine Zahlungsseite noch nicht öffnen. Wenn sie sich nach ein paar Minuten immer noch nicht öffnet, schreib an support@crush.lu mit dem Namen des Events – wir kümmern uns darum.` FR `Nous n'avons pas encore pu ouvrir votre page de paiement. Si elle ne s'ouvre toujours pas après quelques minutes, écrivez à support@crush.lu en indiquant le nom de l'événement : nous nous en occupons.` Same support line on the 500 toast. (3) Add the 500 toast's DE/FR (missing today: S-01, shipped in Phase 4 P4-2 as `.po`-only).
* **Files.** `azure-functions/hybrid-maintenance/function_app.py` (infra: propose only), `views_payments.py` (#1163 file), `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** S-M. Payment-adjacent; claim lifecycle depends on `select_for_update`: untested on Postgres.
* **Metric.** needs data: count of `EventCheckoutCreationClaim` rows in state active older than 10 minutes per week; number of 409s on `/payments/sumup/create-event-checkout/`; support emails "cannot pay".
* **Dependencies.** Sequence after #1163 (`views_payments.py`).
* **Why here.** The only Blocks in Speed Dating: a member who owes money cannot pay and nothing resolves it; frequency unknown, so Medium rather than QW.

##### SD-M2. Say the money rules before the member commits; show credit afterwards (SD-09, SD-05, SD-10, SD-11, XC-11)
* **User problem.** The refund/credit rule appears only on the cancel page and the SumUp widget (`partials/_cancellation_policy_note.html`), not on `event_detail.html` or `event_register.html:50-58`. For curated events with `registration_deadline >= start - 48 h` (the seeded 20/21 are exactly 48 h; live values unknown) every invitee pays inside the no-refund window. No pay-by time exists (SD-05: `emails/event_payment_pending.html:13` says "held for you" while holds never expire and the door admits pending seats). After a cancellation the member sees no balance and no expiry (SD-10: `emails/event_cancellation.html:26` "keeps the applicable credit expiry"; the expiry-reminder command has no timer). A late-cancel resale credit may be too small to buy a seat (SD-11). XC-11: the day-before reminder tells an unpaid member "You're registered" with no payment line.
* **Design.** (1) Reuse `_cancellation_policy_note.html` in the price card, the direct registration page and the pending card. (2) Curated line (verifier fix: the cash option closes once any credit is spent): EN `If your group can't go ahead, we return your payment in full as Crush Credit (card payments can be refunded to your card on request while the credit is unspent).` (3) Owner decision first, then copy: either enforce a hold window or say plainly that paying at the door is allowed; do **not** ship "pay by {deadline}" or "you can also pay at the door" before that (both would mislead today). (4) Per-credit "valid until" dates in the cancel flash, email and a "Crush Credit" line on My Events; never hard-code "6 months" (restored credit keeps its original expiry). (5) SD-11 caveat shipped as `[P4-1]`. (6) Unpaid reminder (verifier's neutral copy): `Payment of {amount} EUR hasn't been received yet. You can pay online from the event page; if you've already arranged payment with us another way, you can ignore this.` (reuses an existing translated sentence).
* **Files.** `event_detail.html`, `event_register.html`, `partials/_cancellation_policy_note.html`, `event_cancel.html`, emails, `views_events.py` context, `my_events.html`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Legal review of ToS 7.1 and 7.3 (curated selection is not mentioned; 7.1 says only approved profiles can register while entry events accept pending ones).
* **Metric.** needs data: configured `registration_deadline` vs start for live curated events; share of curated payers who cancel inside 48 h; pending-unpaid registrations at event start; share of seats paid at the door; credit expiring unspent per month.
* **Dependencies.** `event_register.html` is #1175's file: sequence after #1175. `dashboard.html` is #1163's: put the balance on My Events/account instead.
* **Why here.** Money clarity before commitment is the checklist item most likely to move registration completion and cancellation disputes.

##### SD-M3. Time-critical messages: right time, right words, actually delivered (SD-03, SD-07, SD-16, SD-18)
* **User problem.** SD-03: the day-before web push and the Apple/Google wallet ticket print `event.date_time.strftime(...)` on a UTC datetime: 2 h early in summer (measured: push "21:28", wallet "09:28 PM", site/email "23:28"); late events can also show the wrong date (`push_notifications.py:355-358`, wallet builders). SD-07: the curated invitation and the waitlist promotion reuse the direct-event "Your Spot Is Reserved" mail (`email_helpers.py:876-940`): no group, no "you came off the waitlist". SD-16: invitations go out 10 at a time (`services/curated_group_notifications.py:522`; two groups of six = 12); the rest wait for a second admin click. SD-18: the only push in the journey is the reminder.
* **Design.** (1) `timezone.localtime` in four places and 24 h format for DE/FR (code S). (2) `drain=True` for selection notices as for reserve notices, staff message as a warning. (3) Curated and waitlist branches of the payment-pending mail; verifier fixes: no `{deadline}` unless a hold window is added, "at least five" conversations (the code only guarantees five), the remedy sentence must keep the condition that the cash option closes once credit is spent. (4) New notification types for promotion and invitation (not the existing `EVENT_WAITLIST`/`EVENT_REGISTRATION`, which would re-send the wrong email and mislabel the push), no urgency wording until a deadline exists, split by confirmed vs pending.
* **Files.** `push_notifications.py`, `wallet/*.py`, `services/curated_group_notifications.py`, `admin/events.py`, `email_helpers.py`, `notification_service.py`, templates.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Timezone and pass-rendering changes need a test against the email's `TIME_FORMAT`; notification duplicates.
* **Metric.** needs data: no-shows and "wrong time" complaints per event; selection notices still pending 24 h after an invite action; time from promotion to payment.
* **Dependencies.** `email_helpers.py` is a #1163 file: sequence after.
* **Why here.** SD-03 is a plain bug on the day-of artefact members trust most (no-shows), the rest compounds it.

#### Bold

##### SD-B1. One "where am I?" card for the curated journey (SD-13, SD-12, SD-15, SD-22)
* **User problem.** An applicant who is never selected still reads "Your application is in!" after the evening, and My Events shows a "Recap" button (`event_detail.html:507-516`, `my_events.html:182`; SD-13). A member whose group fell apart after paying sees "No payment was recorded for this registration" on the withdraw page while the email says credit was returned (SD-12, `event_cancel.html:40-42`). "Few of the current applicants match your preferences" shows at a pool of zero and after applying, when preferences cannot be edited (SD-15, `views_events.py:1055-1066`). The withdraw page talks about "your seat" and "payment" to people who hold none (SD-22, unverified).
* **Proposed design.** One status card driven by registration state: applied (with a decision date) -> selected, pay -> group provisional / locked with tables -> "not placed this time" or "credit returned". Closure wording only for unpaid applicants (verifier: "nothing was charged" is false in the SD-12 state, so the card needs a credit-exists guard and the same branches as `curated_group_payment_remedy.html`: reselection possible, cash refund available). The decision date needs an organiser-set field. Soften SD-15 to a neutral "groups are formed after {date}" until the pool is large enough.
* **Files.** `event_detail.html`, `my_events.html`, `event_cancel.html`, `components/curated_group_outlook.html`, `views_events.py`, `models/events.py` (decision date), `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** L. Model field plus several copy branches; SD-12 capture-path locking untested on Postgres.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of curated applicants never selected per evening; time between application deadline and invitations in past events; how often groups degrade after the first payment.
* **Dependencies.** Sequence after #1175 (curated application page, `.po` at EOF); `event_detail.html` is large and shared. #925/#1164 are the Premium analogue, not this path.
* **Why bold.** It turns the curated product's longest silent wait into a visible process.

##### SD-B2. A door-ready ticket that updates itself (SD-21, SD-14)
* **User problem.** After a scan the ticket page says "You're checked in!" only on reload and shows no table or group; a member who scans their own QR with the phone camera lands on an empty 405 page (SD-21, unverified; `event_detail.html:457-459`, `views_ticket.py`). A late or cancelling member finds no contact and no cut-off ("contact your coach" for a first-timer who has none; the guide promises an organiser contact the event page does not list: SD-14, `views_events.py:2292-2304`, `components/event_arrival_guidance.html:13`).
* **Proposed design.** Poll or WebSocket the ticket after check-in; show "Your tables" for locked curated groups; a friendly GET page for the QR URL ("This code is for the coach to scan at the entrance. Just show it on your phone."); one door/late-arrival block (support address now; a phone or WhatsApp number once the owner chooses); for `attended` members the live banner says "You're checked in." instead of "Show your ticket at the door".
* **Files.** `event_ticket.html`, `views_ticket.py`, `event_detail.html` (banner), `components/event_arrival_guidance.html`, `urls`/check-in GET handler, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M-L. WebSocket in production needs ASGI + Redis; table information is personal data (who sits where).
* **Metric.** needs data: late arrivals and no-shows per evening; support emails sent on event days.
* **Dependencies.** None on the open PRs; the cancel-refusal message is already improved by Phase 4 P4-2.
* **Why bold.** It answers the three door-time worries of a nervous first-timer: am I in, where do I sit, did my scan work.

---

### 2. Quiz Night

#### Quick wins

##### QN-QW1. Quiz-night basics in the two emails members actually get (QN-15)  `[P4-3]`
* **User problem.** The confirmation and reminder emails say "What to bring: Valid ID... An open mind... Your best self!" for every event type (`emails/event_registration_confirmation.html:55-66`, `event_reminder.html:50-56`). The one thing a quiz guest needs, a charged phone, and the one thing that happens at the door, check-in assigns your table, appear only on the guide page, which neither email links to.
* **Design.** Quiz-night-only info box in both emails (`event.event_type == "quiz_night"`).
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.** EN: (nothing) -> `For Quiz Night: bring a charged phone. After the coach checks you in you'll get your table number — then tap Join Quiz on the event page. No team needed.` DE: `Für den Quiz-Abend: Bring ein aufgeladenes Handy mit. Nach dem Check-in beim Coach bekommst du deine Tischnummer – dann tippst du auf der Eventseite auf „Quiz beitreten“. Ein eigenes Team brauchst du nicht.` FR: `Pour la soirée quiz : apportez un téléphone chargé. Après votre enregistrement auprès du coach, vous recevrez votre numéro de table ; touchez ensuite « Rejoindre le quiz » sur la page de l'événement. Pas besoin d'équipe.`
* **Files.** the two emails, both `.po`. Optional: same line on the registered-guest event page (`event_detail.html:418-433`) and a link to `/quiz-night/`.
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. "Join Quiz" only works once the host created the quiz (QN-16).
* **Metric.** needs data: guests who need help with phone/table at the door; guests not logged in when the quiz starts.
* **Dependencies.** None. The FR informal "N'oublie pas d'apporter" in the reminder is #1166's family and untouched.
* **Why here.** Highest-leverage Quiz Night copy: it prevents the first-timer's two day-of surprises.

##### QN-QW2. French "Back to Event" says "Back to login" (QN-18)  `[P4-2]`
* **User problem.** FR `Back to Event` ships as "Retour à la connexion" (`django.po:18008`, not fuzzy). It appears on `event_register.html:27`, `event_ticket.html:14`, `quiz_live.html:171` (the only exit from the finished screen), `coach_quiz_config.html:297`, `event_presentations.html:175`, `event_voting_results.html:20`: every French registrant sees it on the money page.
* **Design.** Fix the one FR msgstr (plus two neighbours on the same screens); no template change.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.** FR `Retour à la connexion` -> `Retour à l'événement` (DE `Zurück zum Event` is already right). Same entry family: `Submit Answer` FR `Soumis` ("Submitted") -> `Envoyer la réponse` (visible on Journey challenge pages, not in Quiz Night: verifier correction); `Anchor – stay here` FR `Ancre – reste ici` -> `Ancre – restez ici`.
* **Files.** FR `.po` only.
* **Effort / risk.** S / none. Verifier: drop the reviewer's "checked in -> Arrivés" item; "Enregistré" is the normal French word.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of French-language registrants (URL prefix in access logs).
* **Dependencies.** None. Not covered by #1154/#1166 (shipped-wrong, not fuzzy/empty).
* **Why here.** Pure win on a page that asks for money.

##### QN-QW3. The "Quiz Night Format" box says what the algorithm does (QN-14)
* **User problem.** `event_detail.html:424-431` promises "Teams of 4 at each table (2 men + 2 women)" and "Women rotate between tables after each round". Table size is whoever checks in; roles come from gender (M anchor, F rotator, others join the smaller pool: `quiz_rotation.py:267-279`); a non-binary or prefer-not-to-say guest is not mentioned. Phones and the projector then show "📌 Anchor / 🔄 Rotator" unexplained.
* **Design.** Replace the two format-box lines with the algorithm's actual rule and say where the role badge appears.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER (verifier fix: "assigned automatically at check-in", not "balanced by the coach").**
  * EN: `Teams of 4 at each table (2 men + 2 women)` / `Women rotate between tables after each round` -> `About 4 people per table, assigned automatically at check-in` / `Some guests stay at their table (anchors) and others change tables after each round (rotators). Women rotate; men stay; if you're non-binary or prefer not to say, you're placed where it balances the tables. Your role shows on your phone after check-in.`
  * DE: `Teams von 4 pro Tisch (2 Männer + 2 Frauen)` / `Die Frauen wechseln nach jeder Runde den Tisch` -> `Etwa 4 Personen pro Tisch, beim Check-in automatisch zugewiesen` / `Manche Gäste bleiben an ihrem Tisch (Anker), andere wechseln nach jeder Runde den Tisch (Wechsler). Frauen wechseln, Männer bleiben; wenn du nicht-binär bist oder keine Angabe machst, setzen wir dich so, dass die Tische ausgewogen sind. Deine Rolle siehst du nach dem Check-in auf deinem Handy.`
  * FR: `Équipes de 4 par table (2 hommes + 2 femmes)` / `Les femmes changent de table après chaque manche` -> `Environ 4 personnes par table, réparties automatiquement à l'arrivée` / `Certains invités restent à leur table (ancres), d'autres changent de table après chaque manche (tournants). Les femmes tournent, les hommes restent ; si vous êtes non binaire ou ne souhaitez pas préciser, nous vous plaçons là où les tables sont équilibrées. Votre rôle s'affiche sur votre téléphone après l'arrivée.`
* **Files.** `event_detail.html`, `.po`. Phone label "T3" -> "Table 3" is JS (Medium).
* **Effort / risk.** S / owner sign-off: it publishes the gender-based role rule on a public page (severity Polish; the harm is to non-binary guests). Not in Phase 4 for that reason.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of guests with gender other/blank at past quiz nights; door requests for another role.
* **Dependencies.** None.
* **Why here.** Third: trust for a minority of guests; cheap, but needs an owner's yes.

#### Medium

##### QN-M1. Host panel: mistakes are recoverable (QN-01, QN-07, QN-08, QN-19, QN-13, QN-12)
* **User problem.** One mis-tap on "Confirm End?" ends the quiz for every phone; the only way back is Reset, which always wipes scores (`consumers.py:1341-1346`, `quiz-live.js:1781-1806`; verifier: Hurts, not Blocks, because of the two-tap guard). Every table, including empty ones, must be marked before Next/Rotate and before players see results, and interior empty tables cannot be dissolved (`consumers.py:1393-1400`). A score tap locks (QN-08) while the banner says "...then rotate or select the next round" (selecting rounds is disabled). With a dropped socket every host button is a silent no-op (`quiz-live.js:1725-1783`); the pill says "WebSocket connected". "Update" on Table Setup quietly un-seats guests (`views_quiz_config.py:176-192`). The coach event page has no projector link and its "TV Room Display" card opens the speed-dating screen for quiz events (QN-12).
* **Design.** Allow `finished -> paused` ("Reopen") within a grace period and a "keep scores" reset; count only seated tables for the gate/reveal and label empty cards; undo until the last table is marked (show "marked", not correct/wrong, because the broadcast is deliberately correctness-free before the reveal); disable buttons + banner when disconnected and rename the pill "Live"; warn on Update when seated guests would lose their table and point to "Consolidate Tables (after no-shows)"; on quiz events point the TV card at `quiz_table_display` and show the optional PIN. Copy fixes (verifier): banner -> `All tables are scored. Tap Rotate Tables to start the next round.`; End confirm -> `Tap again to end the quiz for everyone. It can only be reset afterwards, which clears all scores.`; the "Empty tables are skipped" sentence is true only after the gate logic changes.
* **Files.** `consumers.py`, `quiz-live.js`, `quiz_coach.html`, `views_quiz_config.py`, `coach_event_detail.html`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Live-event logic; row locks untested on Postgres.
* **Metric.** needs data: finished -> draft within 10 minutes per quiz; empty tables per quiz night; host support requests.
* **Dependencies.** None.
* **Why here.** A host mistake ruins the evening for 20-40 guests, but only hosts see the buttons: Medium, not QW.

##### QN-M2. The phone tells the truth about the quiz state (QN-03, QN-04, QN-05, QN-10, QN-09, QN-17)
* **User problem.** After every round (5 times in a 6-round pack) phones show "Waiting for the quiz to start..." (`quiz-live.js:506-508`). Pause and reset are ignored, so a reconnect during a pause also shows "Waiting for the quiz to start...". A reconnect after the reveal shows the question again with a running timer and loses "+N pts!"/"Incorrect" (the data is in `scored_tables` but unused). "Move to Table N!", "Stay at your table!", "Incorrect", "Connection lost. Please refresh the page." are hard-coded English under translated headings; after ~8 minutes the page stops retrying while still saying "Reconnecting...". (QN-17, Polish: no instruction on the question screen.)
* **Design.** Dedicated "Round complete" screen (only say "We'll tell you where to sit next" for table-based quiz nights), "Quiz paused"/"Quiz restarting" screens, review screen restored from `scored_tables` + `my_assignment`, strings moved into the `data-i18n` JSON with the DE/FR given by the reviewer (reuse shipped msgids "Incorrect", "Round Complete!"), reconnect on `visibilitychange`/`online`, never give up silently, per-recipient assignment inside `quiz.rotate`.
* **Files.** `quiz-live.js`, `quiz_live.html`, `consumers.py`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. JS plus consumer payload; real sockets and Redis not exercised locally.
* **Metric.** needs data: WebSocket connects between reveal and next question; median offline time and close counts per night; share of DE/FR phones.
* **Dependencies.** #1154 overlaps only on the legacy "+N points" string.
* **Why here.** Guests see this every round; the wrong-language rotate instruction is the most time-critical text on the phone.

##### QN-M3. No more "page doesn't exist" at the venue (QN-06, QN-11, QN-16, QN-20, QN-02)
* **User problem.** A registered guest who scans the projector QR before the coach scanned their ticket gets a 404 "We couldn't find that page" (`views_quiz.py:193-198`). A checked-in guest with no seat sees no table and no message (QN-11, Polish). "Join Quiz" shows when no quiz exists (404) and after the quiz ended (`models/events.py:1273-1280`). A coach who is not assigned to the event gets a 404 from the green "Quiz Host Panel" button (`views_quiz.py:279-286`). A guest who checks in after round 0 earns no points for the rest of the round and nobody is told (QN-02: verifier narrowed it to check-ins after round 0).
* **Design.** Keep every access rule; change only what is shown: a 200 "You're not checked in yet" page (verifier fix: it must say "reload this page once the coach has checked you in", or auto-refresh; a static page does not open itself), "You don't have a table yet. Ask the coach..." for unseated guests, show Join Quiz only when a `QuizEvent` exists and relabel "See quiz results" after finish, an explanation instead of the host-panel 404, "You joined mid-round..." when the current-round schedule row is missing.
* **Files.** `views_quiz.py`, `models/events.py`, `quiz_live.html`, `coach_event_detail.html`, `dashboard.html` + `views.py` (QN-16 dashboard row), `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Presentation only for the access rules.
* **Metric.** needs data: 404s on `/events/<id>/quiz/` for authenticated users; attended registrations without a table membership at quiz time; quiz_night events that reach their date without a `QuizEvent`.
* **Dependencies.** QN-16 touches `dashboard.html` and `views.py`: sequence after #1163.
* **Why here.** Dead ends at the venue, but each hits a small share of guests.

#### Bold

##### QN-B1. **Close the open projector data endpoint and say who sees what** (security/privacy review needed; do not implement)
* **User problem.** `GET /api/quiz/<event_id>/display-data/` answers **anonymously** with each guest's display name, role, table, scores and photo URL whenever `QuizEvent.display_token` is blank, which is the default (model help text: "Leave blank for unrestricted access"; `models/quiz.py:187-195`, `views_quiz.py:420-470`). The review coordinator confirmed it with curl; QN-21's verifier got HTTP 200 with keys `tables, attended_count, quiz_status, leaderboard_tables, leaderboard_individuals` and no login (photos stay login-gated). Event ids are sequential. Names are first names, or full names for members who ticked "Show my full name". No page tells guests that a venue screen shows them (QN-21; `quiz_display.html:74-127`).
* **Proposed design.** (1) Generate a token by default when a quiz is created (and at start for existing quizzes), carry it in the projector URL and its QR, require it on the JSON endpoint, or require an attendee/coach session. (2) Return first names and table numbers only. (3) Disclose at registration and on the ticket, using the verifier's wording: EN `The venue screen shows each guest's name (first name unless you chose to show your full name), table and score.` DE `Auf dem Bildschirm vor Ort siehst du Namen (Vorname, außer du hast den vollen Namen freigegeben), Tisch und Punktestand aller Gäste.` FR `L'écran de la salle affiche le nom (prénom, sauf si vous avez choisi d'afficher votre nom complet), la table et le score de chaque invité.` Do not promise "only on the venue screen" until the endpoint is gated. (4) Same review: the `quiz.rotate` broadcast carries every attendee's internal user id, name, table and role to every phone (`consumers.py:1865-1893`, while `quiz_table_update` deliberately omits ids), and `/api/quiz/<id>/state/` + `/my-assignment/` need only a login (`api_quiz.py:51-60, 188`).
* **Files.** `views_quiz.py`, `models/quiz.py` (default token), `quiz-display.js`, `coach_event_detail.html` (tokenised projector link, see QN-M1), `consumers.py`, `api_quiz.py`, `event_register.html`/`event_ticket.html` (disclosure; `event_register.html` is #1175's file).
* **Effort / risk.** M. Permission change: owner and security review; the coach must receive the tokenised projector URL; existing quizzes need a migration path.
* **Metric.** needs data: access-log hits on `/api/quiz/*/display-data/` without a token and from non-attendee clients; whether the projector device is normally signed in as a coach (whether photos are shown).
* **Dependencies.** None on the open PRs (disclosure line on the registration page: sequence after #1175).
* **Why bold.** The largest trust exposure in the review: personal data of attendees of a dating event readable without an account.

##### QN-B2. Quiz night as a first-class event: language-aware, readiness-checked, with a souvenir (QN-25, QN-23, QN-24; unverified Polish)
* **User problem.** Real quiz nights are language-specific ("de/lu" or "fr", `create_september_2026_events.py:179-188`) but neither the phone nor the projector reads `event.languages`; the join QR is built in the projector's language, so a French guest scanning an English projector gets English (`views_quiz.py:361-363`); the readiness check covers rounds, questions, tables, photos but not DE/FR text or the projector (`models/quiz.py:273-413`); the finished screen's "Back to Event" does not say when connections open (at the scheduled end, not when the quiz finishes) and the recap email has no score or rank (QN-23); small `text-gray-500` host text is estimated at 3.0-3.7:1 contrast (QN-24, estimate only).
* **Proposed design.** Readiness lines ("Translations: {a}/{b} questions have DE and FR text"), a language chip on the stage that switches in place, default language from `event.languages`, a finished-screen line "Connections open at {time} — then you can tell us who you'd like to meet again", and a recap line with table rank and score; fix the contrast tokens after an axe run.
* **Files.** `models/quiz.py`, `views_quiz.py`, `quiz_display.html`, `quiz_live.html`, `quiz-live.js`, `emails/event_recap.html`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** L. Not adversarially verified; needs a spec and the language data.
* **Metric.** needs data: attendee `preferred_language` vs the URL language used; which language prefix hosts open the projector in; recap open/click and My Crush! starts after quiz nights; axe contrast results (not run: the sandbox serves no CSS).
* **Dependencies.** #1154 (points/votes plurals), #1165 (a11y follow-ups); `emails/event_recap.html` is also touched by Phase 4 P4-3 (sequence after it).
* **Why bold.** It makes Quiz Night behave like the multilingual, event-specific product it is sold as.
