### 3. Crush Connect

#### Quick wins

##### CC-QW1. Make the promise match the product: who arranges the date, and how many profiles a day (CC-06, CC-20)  `[P4-1]`
* **User problem.** CC-06: the marketing page's step 4 says "your Crush Coach contacts you both and arranges the date personally" (`crush_connect.html:184`), but a free mutual yes only opens a temporary chat with a coffee-plan form (`services/connect_cycle.py:824-837`); only the Premium Coach's Pick has a coach arranging the date. Wizard steps 1-2 repeat "your coach..." for members who have no coach. CC-20: the readiness checklist says "3 suggestions each day" in three strings (`_readiness_checklist.html:11`, `views_crush_connect.py:176, 237`); the real value is `CARDS_PER_DAY = 2` (`services/connect_cycle.py:56-57`) and the dashboard and beta email already say two.
* **Design.** Replace six English strings (and their DE/FR) so the marketing step, the wizard subtitles and the checklist describe the free chat flow and the real card count.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.**
  * EN: `When it's mutual, your Crush Coach contacts you both and arranges the date personally.` -> `When it's mutual, a private chat opens so you can plan a coffee together. Premium members also get their coach's weekly pick, and the coach arranges that date.`
  * DE: `Wenn es beidseitig ist, kontaktiert Dein Crush Coach euch beide und organisiert das Date persönlich.` -> `Wenn es beidseitig ist, öffnet sich ein privater Chat, in dem ihr gemeinsam einen Kaffee plant. Premium-Mitglieder erhalten zusätzlich die wöchentliche Auswahl ihres Coachs – dieses Date arrangiert der Coach.`
  * FR: `Quand c'est réciproque, votre Crush Coach vous contacte tous les deux et organise le rendez-vous personnellement.` -> `Si c'est réciproque, une discussion privée s'ouvre pour organiser un café ensemble. Les membres Premium reçoivent en plus la proposition hebdomadaire de leur coach, qui organise ce rendez-vous.`
  * Wizard step 1, EN `No wrong answers — your coach just needs a starting point.` -> `No wrong answers — we just need a starting point.` DE `...wir brauchen nur einen Ausgangspunkt.` FR `Il n'y a pas de mauvaises réponses — nous avons juste besoin d'un point de départ.` Step 2, EN `These help your coach find someone whose rhythm matches yours.` -> `These help us find someone whose rhythm matches yours.` DE `Das hilft uns, jemanden zu finden, dessen Rhythmus zu deinem passt.` FR `Cela nous aide à trouver quelqu'un dont le rythme correspond au vôtre.`
  * Checklist, EN `To receive your 3 suggestions each day, complete these steps` -> `To receive your 2 new profiles each day, complete these steps`; DE `Um täglich 2 neue Profile zu erhalten, schließe diese Schritte ab`; FR `Pour recevoir 2 nouveaux profils par jour, complétez ces étapes` (plus the two sentences at `views_crush_connect.py:176, 237`).
* **Files.** `crush_connect.html`, `_step1_intention.html`, `_step2_lifestyle.html`, `_readiness_checklist.html`, `views_crush_connect.py`, `.po`. Better follow-up: build the number from `CARDS_PER_DAY`.
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Verifier: `:99` and `experiences/coach-pick.html:22/46` are about the Premium pick, where the claim is true; only `:184` is wrong.
* **Metric.** needs data: Connect onboarding completion; accepted chats that never get a message (expectation gap).
* **Dependencies.** None (#1166 edits other lines of `crush_connect.html`).
* **Why here.** First: a false promise on the one page the whole audience reads.

##### CC-QW2. Label the review rows as the viewer's own guesses (CC-15)  `[P4-1]`
* **User problem.** In the 24-hour review each card lists `“Do I work in finance?” — Yes`; the Yes/No is the viewer's earlier guess, but the questions are phrased in the other person's first person, so on a phone it reads as their answer (`_review_card.html:56-60`). This is the page where the one request of the week is chosen.
* **Design.** Prefix each review row's Yes/No with "Your guess:" and add one intro line; no layout change.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER (verifier fix: FR must not say "répondu").**
  * EN: `“Do I work in finance?” — Yes` -> `“Do I work in finance?” — Your guess: Yes`; top line `These are your guesses, not their answers. Their answers stay private.`
  * DE: `„Arbeite ich im Finanzwesen?“ – Dein Tipp: Ja`; `Das sind deine Tipps, nicht ihre Antworten. Ihre Antworten bleiben privat.`
  * FR: `« Est-ce que je travaille dans la finance ? » — Votre supposition : Oui`; `Ce sont vos suppositions, pas leurs réponses. Leurs réponses restent privées.` (also fixes the shipped FR "Vos réponses restent privées" on the card to "Vos suppositions...").
* **Files.** `_review_card.html`, `week_review.html`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Verifier: "trust and safety risk" is overstated (the guess never leaks the target's answer); the harm is misreading a guess as a fact.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of requests sent to the highlighted suggestion; requests sent per review.
* **Dependencies.** None.
* **Why here.** Second: it guards the product's one high-stakes decision at almost no cost.

##### CC-QW3. Consent, card button and the safety path in German and French (CC-02, CC-18)  `[P4-4]`
* **User problem.** The two REQUIRED consent boxes on onboarding step 7 render in English on `/de/` and `/fr/` (`forms_crush_connect.py:394-408`: one EMPTY, one FUZZY with a stale msgstr about a blurred-photo card); the Connect Week card button "Answer", "You've read X today." and the card's aria-label have no `.po` entry. On People I've Met the whole removal/safety block ("Hide or remove this person", "I feel unsafe or uncomfortable", flash "This person is now hidden...") is English or a fuzzy-wrong guess (`event_lobby/person_profile.html:65-101`, `lobby_locked.html`, `lobby_closed.html`, bell text in `services/event_lobby.py`).
* **Design.** Fill the EMPTY entries, replace the FUZZY ones, add the three missing msgids, rename one button; the English consent text stays as it is.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.**
  * DE consent: (English shown) -> `Ich bin einverstanden, dass mein Foto den wenigen Personen, die mir täglich vorgeschlagen werden, deutlich gezeigt wird, damit sie meine Fragen erraten können.` FR: `J'accepte que ma photo soit montrée en clair, chaque jour, aux quelques personnes à qui je suis proposé(e), afin qu'elles puissent deviner mes réponses.`
  * Button, EN `Answer` -> `Save my guesses`; DE `Tipps speichern`; FR `Enregistrer mes suppositions`.
  * Safety, DE `Person ausblenden oder entfernen` / `Ich fühle mich unsicher oder unwohl`; FR `Masquer ou retirer cette personne` / `Je me sens en insécurité ou mal à l'aise`.
* **Files.** `.po` + `_cycle_card.html:94`. Full list in the fix PR descriptions (Section 5) P4-4.
* **Effort / risk.** S / consent text: have the owner or legal glance at the DE/FR consent (CC-04 shows the English "matched with me" is itself inaccurate; the interim DE/FR say "to whom I am proposed"). The English is not changed here.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of Connect members using DE/FR; prod value of `CRUSH_EVENT_LOBBY_ENABLED` (lobby strings only show when it is on).
* **Dependencies.** None. #1166 covers `recap.html` only; #1154 contains the fuzzy subset.
* **Why here.** Third: informed consent and a safety path in a language the member may not read.

#### Medium

##### CC-M1. Premium: the advertised price and the charged price differ (CC-01 Blocks, CC-12, CO-12)
* **User problem.** "First month free" ships on at least five surfaces (`crush_connect.html:208`, `partials/_products.html:110/137`, `experiences/coach-pick.html:60`, `partials/_verification_options.html:132/134`; "Cancel anytime" also on `about.html:343`, `includes/cta_section.html:60`), but checkout charges the full monthly fee immediately with `SETUP_RECURRING_PAYMENT` and no trial branch exists (`views_payments.py:979-980, 1038`). `membership.html:29` even carries the comment "no trial is promised". The price first appears after a coach is chosen (`premium/choose_coach.html:48`). There is no member path to end an ACTIVE membership (`cancel_active` has one caller, the reconcile command). Verifier: nothing in the repo bills month 2, so the real gap is a promise ("a month", "cancel anytime") the code does not operate. CC-12/CO-12: with `PREMIUM_REDIRECTS_TO_BETA` (default True) "Go Premium - first month free" silently redirects non-testers to a page about something else; where it lands depends on the member's state.
* **Design.** Step 1 (copy): remove "first month free" everywhere until a trial exists; show price and billing before the coach choice (reuse the `membership.html` msgid `€{{ amount }} / month`; no hard "per month" claim until renewal is confirmed); an honest CTA while in beta ("Premium is invite-only during the beta — join the waitlist" / DE `Premium ist während der Beta nur auf Einladung verfügbar – setz dich auf die Warteliste` / FR `Premium est disponible sur invitation pendant la bêta — rejoignez la liste d'attente`); a flash on the redirect that does not point at a location (verifier: the landing page varies). Step 2, decisions to propose: a real first-month trial or retire the claim; a self-service "Cancel Premium" for ACTIVE members behind a confirm sheet; until then "Cancel anytime by contacting support".
* **Files.** `crush_connect.html`, `_products.html`, `_verification_options.html`, `experiences/coach-pick.html`, `choose_coach.html`, `views_premium.py`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M; payment-adjacent, so copy first.
* **Metric.** needs data: is a free first month granted outside the repo (SumUp merchant config or a staff confirm)? prod values of `SUMUP_PREMIUM_MONTHLY_FEE` and `PREMIUM_REDIRECTS_TO_BETA`; funnel choose-coach -> payment; refund/chargeback requests. If a free month is granted, CC-01 drops to Hurts (verifier).
* **Dependencies.** **Sequence after #1163** (touches `_products.html`, `_verification_options.html`, `choose_coach.html`, `membership.html`, `views_premium.py`; its hunks do not touch the "first month free" lines) and after #925/#1164 (captured-not-applied, different problem).
* **Why here.** The only Blocks in Connect and the highest-trust moment (money); exposure is small today (selected testers) and total at launch.

##### CC-M2. Say the rules before the one weekly request (CC-07, CC-03)
* **User problem.** The sender's dialog says only "You cannot choose someone else this week after sending" (`_review_card.html:73-83`). Not said: 24 h to answer, chat on yes, silence on no, permanent exclusion on decline or expiry (`services/connect_cycle.py:676-687, 838-846`). A decline reads "They weren't able to connect right now." (implies a retry that never comes); the recipient's "Yes, let's chat" has no consequence line and ignoring is not explained as expiry-equals-decline (`week_inbox.html:52-57`); the hub "Your response needed" card has no deadline. CC-03: a member who pauses with a request waiting cannot answer it, is not warned (`pause_confirm.html:13-24`), and the sender's review says "You didn't answer any cards this cycle" although the card was completed (`week_review.html:62-65`).
* **Design.** Dialog, outcome lines, inbox line, email line and failure toast as in the reviewer's copy, with verifier scope fixes: the "you won't be shown to each other again" line is scoped to Connect Week; the inbox already shows "Response deadline" (only the hub card lacks it); sender-side silence on declines is intentional, so decline and expiry share one text. Pause screen: warn when a request is waiting (`get_pending_inbox`); fix the review wording when completed cards are hidden because the target paused. Propose only: do not write a permanent exclusion when the recipient was paused at expiry.
* **Files.** `_review_card.html`, `week_review.html`, `week_inbox.html`, `emails/connect_week_request.html`, `pause_confirm.html`, `views_connect_cycle.py`, `connect_summary` (hub deadline: split the PR if shipping copy only), `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** S-M. Untested on Postgres (request-state locks).
* **Metric.** needs data: share of requests that expire unanswered today (the code comment's "17 of 24" predates email+push); `ConnectWeeklyRequest` rows that ended EXPIRED where the recipient had `paused_at` set.
* **Dependencies.** None.
* **Why here.** The product's one high-stakes action; copy-heavy and cheap but not copy-only after the verifier's corrections.

##### CC-M3. Every locked state ends with one reason and one next step (CC-05, CC-08, CC-11, CC-19, CC-22)
* **User problem.** Every Connect gate returns the same silent redirect to the marketing page (`views_crush_connect.py:124-133, 319-333, 795-796`); whose status block is section 6 of 8. The hero button "Open Crush Connect" points back at the page you are on. A member with no profile, an incomplete one, or one pending review is told "Verify your identity to join Crush Connect" and offered a waitlist (verifier: the approved-but-unverified member does get a reason, just far down). Event-verified members without LuxID get a 404 from the Event Lobby and a promo for Crush Connect, members who paused or revoked photo consent get no reason (`services/event_lobby.py:153, 884-898`). A Premium member before onboarding sees a coach name and a checklist about a different promise, then "Your coach is choosing carefully" with no cadence, and the flash promises a date while the page says one is arranged only if both agree (CC-11). "Explore ->" links on the marketing page hit a login wall (CC-19). Waitlist position `#14` implies a queue that does not exist (CC-22).
* **Design.** A `messages.info` before each locked redirect and a state-specific headline at the top of the teaser for signed-in visitors; lobby: pass the member-fixable reason through `lobby_cta` ("needs LuxID", "your Connect is paused", "photo sharing is off"; keep coach exclusion generic; verifier: drop "about a minute"); Premium: "{coach} can start choosing for you once your Connect profile is complete. {n} steps left." and an honest empty state; flash `Done — your coach will ask them and let you know. A date is arranged only if you both agree.`; relabel Explore for visitors or (permission change, propose only) make the three explainers public. Verifier fixes: do not claim beta places are "chosen by hand" for Connect Week (they open by verification; only the tester flag is hand-picked), say what the list is for and what the member will receive.
* **Files.** `views_crush_connect.py`, `views_static.py`, `crush_connect.html`, `services/event_lobby.py`, `event_lobby/lobby_locked.html`, `hub.html`, `coach_pick.html`, `_waitlist_join.html`, `connect.js`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Accepting event-verified members in `participant_gate` is a permission decision: propose only.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of authenticated `/crush-connect/home/` and `/onboarding/` hits that bounce to the teaser; checked-in attendees who are Connect-onboarded but fail the lobby gate, by reason; waitlist joiners who are not yet approved; median days from onboarding to first pick.
* **Dependencies.** `hub.html` and Premium pages: check #1163 (not on its list, `views_premium.py` is).
* **Why here.** Locked states are where first-timers conclude the product is broken.

#### Bold

##### CC-B1. A notification rhythm for Connect Week (CC-10, CC-16, CC-17)
* **User problem.** Connect Week is built on clocks (daily cards, 24 h review, 24 h request, 7-day chat) but only "request received" goes through `NotificationService` with email, push and bell in the recipient's language (`notification_service.py:444-453`). "It's mutual!" is a bell only and its text is rendered in the accepter's language (`services/connect_cycle.py:919-933`); nothing tells a member the review opened or is about to close; new chat messages produce no push or email; `ConnectTemporaryChat.reminder_sent` is never set; a missed day means no cards, and an empty day gives no reason (CC-16, `week_home.html:76`). The chat header says "Closes Mon 23:34" with no explanation (CC-17, `chat_detail.html:35`).
* **Proposed design.** Push + email (respecting `new_connections`) for review open, request accepted, new message (throttled) and "chat closes tomorrow" (verifier: after both confirm they met the expiry is a fixed +3 days and messages no longer extend it); every bell row written with `translation.override(recipient language)`; an empty-day reason with a link to the preference section (`?section=ideal_match` is valid; soften "the group is still small" to "not enough new people match your preferences yet"); chat chip with date and the 7-day rule; collapse the coffee-plan form below the thread on phones.
* **Files.** `notification_service.py`, `services/connect_cycle.py`, `services/connect_chat.py`, a timer command + `azure-functions` (propose only), `week_home.html`, `chat_detail.html`, `connect-live-chat.js`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** L. New notification types, a sweep timer, notification fatigue; the Connect WhatsApp template is env-gated.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of reviews first opened more than 12 h after the cycle ended; accepted chats with zero messages after 48 h; share of chats that close by inactivity; share of member-days with 0 cards.
* **Dependencies.** #1147 is unrelated. Generalised in Cross-cutting XC-B2.
* **Why bold.** The best moment in the product (a mutual yes) reaches the sender only as a bell.

##### CC-B2. From "we met" to "let's meet again" (CC-09, CC-08)
* **User problem.** Two members confirm they met at an event and People I've Met ends at "A permanent record of a real-world meeting. Say hi next time you see them." (`event_lobby/person_profile.html:101`): no message, no coach, no Connect request. For pairs inside an open recap the My Crush! button is replaced by "Find them in your event recap" (`event_attendees.html:67-72`), while the same page still promises that a coach will arrange an introduction. Event-verified members without LuxID get a 404 from the lobby (`services/event_lobby.py:153`).
* **Proposed design.** Decide one next step: restore My Crush! for confirmed pairs, or add a private one-sided action consistent with My Crush!. Until then stop implying a relationship tool in the lobby promo ("keep the people you both confirm"). Verifier: the reviewer's "Want your coach to help you stay in touch? [Tell my coach]" advertises a feature that does not exist and cannot ship as copy. Separately decide whether coach-verified attendance should open the lobby (permission change: propose only).
* **Files.** `event_lobby/person_profile.html`, `event_attendees.html`, `services/event_lobby.py` (`crush_flow_decision`, `participant_gate`), `components/event_lobby_promo.html`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** L. Product and permission decisions; privacy.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of confirmed encounters followed by a My Crush! or Connect request within 7 days; how many recap-eligible pairs previously used My Crush!.
* **Dependencies.** CO-15 wording; #1167 mentions only the "Contact Info Shared" label.
* **Why bold.** It decides whether the Event Lobby produces second meetings or only a list.

---

### 4. Crush Coach and the Coach Panel

#### Quick wins

##### CO-QW1. The rejection email stops contradicting the rejection page (CO-03)  `[P4-3]`
* **User problem.** The email says "You may create a new profile addressing the concerns mentioned above" (`emails/profile_rejected.html:23`); the web page says "It cannot be resubmitted" and `views_profile.py:725-736` blocks resubmission, and a rejected account is redirected to `/profile/rejected/`. A member following the email creates a second profile and hits the wall again, or contacts nobody. Door rejections use the same email.
* **Design.** Delete one list item from the email; keep the contact-support bullet.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.** Remove the bullet. EN `You may create a new profile addressing the concerns mentioned above` / DE `Du kannst ein neues Profil erstellen, das die oben genannten Bedenken berücksichtigt` / FR `Vous pouvez créer un nouveau profil en tenant compte des préoccupations mentionnées ci-dessus` -> (line removed). The remaining bullet keeps its translated msgid: EN `If you believe this was an error, please contact us at support@crush.lu` / DE `Wenn du glaubst, dass es sich hierbei um einen Fehler handelte, kontaktiere uns bitte unter support@crush.lu` / FR `Si vous pensez qu'il s'agit d'une erreur, veuillez nous contacter à support@crush.lu`. Optional warmer wording: `If you think this is a mistake, write to us at support@crush.lu — we'll look at it with you.` / `Wenn du glaubst, dass das ein Fehler ist, schreib uns an support@crush.lu – wir schauen es uns gemeinsam mit dir an.` / `Si vous pensez qu'il s'agit d'une erreur, écrivez-nous à support@crush.lu : nous l'examinerons avec vous.`
* **Files.** `emails/profile_rejected.html`. Verifier addition: the opening "we're unable to approve your profile at this time" also reads wrongly for an automatic door rejection ("at this time" suggests temporary).
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Whether a second account is allowed stays an owner policy call.
* **Metric.** needs data: door rejections per event and how many are later reversed; support emails from rejected members.
* **Dependencies.** None. Distinct from CO-01/XC-01 (Coach M1).
* **Why here.** First: two official messages giving opposite next steps to a member in the most fragile state.

##### CO-QW2. The My Crush! privacy promise says exactly what happens (CO-15)
* **User problem.** The declarer is told "Your crush stays completely private — they are never notified" (`request_connection.html:67`, `event_attendees.html:194`, `_request_connection_form.html`, `connection_detail.html:162`, `_connection_request_success.html:10`, the flash in `views_connections.py`). The recipient's own coach is told "Your member was named in a My Crush! declaration. Reach out to them and record their answer here." (`coach_crush_outreach_task.html:22`). The system sends no automatic notice, but the recipient is always contacted. Five of these six places are untranslated in DE/FR.
* **Design.** The same exact sentence in all six places and in DE/FR, once the owner confirms the identity-reveal rule.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER (owner must confirm when identities are revealed; verifier: say "will check with them first" only if the recipient is always contacted).**
  * EN: `Your crush stays completely private — they are never notified` -> `Your crush is never told it was you unless they agree to an introduction. Their own coach will check with them first, without saying who you are.`
  * DE: `Dein Crush bleibt völlig privat — die Person wird nie benachrichtigt` -> `Dein Crush erfährt nicht, dass du es warst, außer er oder sie stimmt einer Vorstellung zu. Sein oder ihr Coach fragt vorher nach, ohne zu sagen, wer du bist.`
  * FR: `Votre coup de cœur reste totalement privé — la personne n'est jamais notifiée` -> `Votre coup de cœur ne saura pas que c'est vous, sauf s'il ou elle accepte une présentation. Son coach lui en parlera d'abord, sans dire qui vous êtes.`
* **Files.** the six templates/flash + `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** S, owner decision. Not in Phase 4: do it after P4-4 (which translates today's English) or update DE/FR in the same change.
* **Metric.** needs data: complaints from recipients; declarations per event before and after.
* **Dependencies.** P4-4 (same strings).
* **Why here.** Second: a privacy promise in a dating product must be exact; cheap once the owner answers one question.

##### CO-QW3. The Coach Panel in German and French: the door and My Crush! call screens (CO-20, unverified)
* **User problem.** I re-ran the scan: 11 msgids on `coach_event_checkin.html` have no `.po` entry in either language ("Photo mismatch", its destructive confirm "Reject verification for this attendee? Their profile will be marked unverified due to photo mismatch.", "Could not reject verification", "Undo check-in", "Print", "Reprint Ticket", "Printer: ON/OFF", "Test Bon", the two thermal-printer lines), and the finding counts 28 more coach-panel entries with an EMPTY msgstr (co-coach outreach task, the 48 h SLA text, the My Crush! reminder push). Coaches are recruited as "at least 2 of EN, DE, FR".
* **Design.** Add the 11 absent msgids and fill the empty coach-panel entries in DE/FR; route the JS literals through gettext in a follow-up.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.** `Photo mismatch` -> DE `Foto stimmt nicht` / FR `Photo non conforme`; the reject confirm -> DE `Verifizierung für diese Person ablehnen? Ihr Profil wird wegen nicht passender Fotos als nicht verifiziert markiert.` / FR `Refuser la vérification de cette personne ? Son profil sera marqué non vérifié car les photos ne correspondent pas.`; `Undo check-in` -> `Check-in rückgängig machen` / `Annuler l'enregistrement`; `Print` -> `Drucken` / `Imprimer`; My Crush! reminder push `My Crush! call still open` -> DE `My Crush!-Anruf noch offen` / FR `Appel My Crush! toujours en attente`.
* **Files.** `.po`; JS literals in `coach.js:76-98, 106-108` ("Start Scanner", "Live", "Offline") need routing through gettext (separate).
* **Effort / risk.** S; **not adversarially verified**, so not in Phase 4. Register: coach-facing DE is du here but one string uses "Sie" (owner decision, see Section 7.4).
* **Metric.** needs data: coach language mix; how often "Photo mismatch" is used.
* **Dependencies.** None (#1176 touches `views_coach.py` and one test only).
* **Why here.** Third: the highest-stakes door action and its confirmation are English-only for DE/FR coaches.

#### Medium

##### CO-M1. A door-rejected member is not accused, looped or told to browse (CO-01 + XC-01, merged: same root cause)
* **User problem.** CO-01 (Blocks, kept because it locks the member out of every entry event and accuses them of breaking the Terms) and XC-01 (verifier: Hurts) describe one chain. After a photo mismatch at the door: the profile page says "Your profile does not comply with our Terms of Service and has not been approved. It cannot be resubmitted." (`profile_rejected.html:23`; for a door rejection `submission` is None so no reason is shown); the dashboard says "Profile not approved — Please contact support" directly above "No upcoming events yet / Browse upcoming events" and the "Support Crush.lu" donation card (`dashboard.html:259-263`); the event page says "Finish your profile to register" with [Complete Profile] (`event_detail.html:938`), which redirects to the rejected page. Only the `completed` requirement branch lacks the rejected card its sibling branches show (`event_detail.html:715-731`). `views_checkin.py:105` lets a coach re-verify a rejected profile at the door, but the member cannot register for an event where that could happen. The door reason exists only in the email and bell (`views_checkin.py:1033-1034`). The page also says "Your PowerUp account remains active for other platforms" (`profile_rejected.html:60`; the GDPR page says "login account").
* **Design.** (1) Event page: reuse the existing rejected block (`Your profile was not approved, so you cannot register for this event. Please contact support.` + Contact support, already translated) in the `completed` branch. (2) Rejected page: say what happened only once the reason is stored (verifier: the page cannot tell door from ToS rejection today; do **not** ship "so a coach can verify you in person" because no self-serve or registration path exists); until then replace "PowerUp account" by "login account" (DE `Dein Login-Konto bleibt aktiv.` / FR `Votre compte de connexion reste actif.`). (3) Dashboard: no browse-events invitation and no donation card for rejected members (needs a context flag). (4) Propose only: store the door-rejection reason on the profile; decide whether a door-rejected member may re-attempt at a later entry event.
* **Files.** `event_detail.html`, `profile_rejected.html`, `dashboard.html`, `views.py` (both #1163), `views_checkin.py`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Verification policy; do not claim "not a ban" without the owner's confirmation.
* **Metric.** needs data: door rejections per event and how many are later reversed (false-positive rate); count of door-rejected vs ToS-rejected profiles; support emails from rejected members.
* **Dependencies.** `dashboard.html` and `views.py` are #1163 files: sequence after.
* **Why here.** Highest severity in the review (Blocks), small population, severe harm.

##### CO-M2. The door screen shows who needs what (CO-02, CO-04, CO-05, CO-06, CO-13, CO-18, CO-19)
* **User problem.** A door scan skips profiles without a photo (`views_checkin.py:318`) and the member's dashboard then still says "no further action needed before then" (`_verification_journey.html:132-133`). The roster row of an unpaid `pending` seat is identical to a paid one (`coach_event_checkin.html:234`; the member's own ticket warns). The live row never gets the "Photo mismatch" button (`coach.js:664`; only a 5 s toast carries it; the approval email goes out before anyone compares the face). Pills are binary: a previously rejected person, a photo-less member and an incomplete profile all read "Unverified [Verify] [Check In]", 4 px apart. The scanner is three taps from login (`coach_event_detail.html:120`). Offline taps raise a blocking `alert("Network error")`; scan results are raw English f-strings (CO-18/19, unverified).
* **Design.** Payment chip reusing the shipped msgid `Payment due — €%(amount)s`; render "Photo mismatch" in `_buildActions`; differentiate pills with the shipped msgids "No photo" and "Rejected" (verifier: say "Rejected", not "Rejected at a previous event"; showing a prior rejection to volunteer coaches is a privacy/policy call); gap or stacking at 390 px; "Tonight: {event} — Open check-in" shortcut on the dashboard; toasts instead of alerts, an honest "Offline" chip; member-side: add-a-photo nudge and an "attended but not verified" state (gate the "or connect LuxID" clause with `{% luxid_available %}`).
* **Files.** `coach_event_checkin.html`, `coach.js`, `views_checkin.py`, `coach_dashboard.html`, `coach_event_detail.html`, `_verification_journey.html` (member side), `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Live coach UI; measured at 390x844 in Chromium for CO-04/05/06.
* **Metric.** needs data: door arrivals with status pending/unpaid per paid event; share of check-ins where the profile has no photo; how often "Photo mismatch" is used and how long after check-in; door-night failure rate (error rates on `POST /api/events/checkin/`).
* **Dependencies.** `_verification_journey.html` is a #1163 file. `coach_event_detail.html` is #1176's *page* but the PR only changes counting in `views_coach.py`; no line overlap with the Event Control card.
* **Why here.** The door is where verification and money actually happen; every one of these rows is a silent failure.

##### CO-M3. Make the coach relationship real (CO-07, CO-08, CO-09, CO-10)
* **User problem.** First attendance silently grants a permanent coach (`signals.py:4199`), yet the dashboard "Your Crush Coach" card only renders for a legacy submission (`views.py:463`) and the pricing table says "✕ No personal coach". "My Members" lists only profiles with an APPROVED submission, so it is empty for every post-pivot coach (`views_coach.py:1322-1331`; the coach dashboard meanwhile counts approved profiles platform-wide). The 48-hour My Crush! call rests on a push reminder that is stamped delivered when no coach device is opted in (`services/crush_leads.py:153-177`), gated by a toggle labelled "Screening Reminders" that coaches would naturally mute. The campaign audience "Users awaiting coach approval" resolves via the legacy queue (5 rows locally vs 14 pending profiles).
* **Design.** Verifier-safe first step for CO-07: let the dashboard verifier fall back to `profile.assigned_coach` for coach-event-verified members (existing "Verified by your coach" card); notify and show contact only after the owner approves exposing a coach's address (`mailto:` today). CO-08: back the roster by `assigned_coach` after confirming that is the intended meaning, with the empty state (verifier fix) `New members you check in at an event are assigned to you if they have no coach yet.` CO-09: rename/re-scope the toggle ("Call reminders"), email or in-app fallback when a coach has zero opted-in devices, DE/FR for the reminder push; escalation after 48 h is notification policy: propose only. CO-10: re-point `pending_review` to `verification_status='pending'` (needs a choices migration and a check of existing Newsletter rows).
* **Files.** `views.py`, `dashboard.html`, `_products.html` (#1163), `views_coach.py`, `coach_members.html`, `services/crush_leads.py`, `coach_notifications.py`, `edit_account_notifications.html`, `newsletter_service.py`, `models/newsletter.py`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Product-model question first: what does `assigned_coach` mean to the member? (verifier: Crush Connect deliberately excludes attendance-assigned coaches from curation).
* **Metric.** needs data: share of active coaches with an enabled `CoachPushSubscription` and `notify_screening_reminders=True`; crush leads called after 48 h; production count of ProfileSubmission pending vs CrushProfile pending.
* **Dependencies.** #1163 (`views.py`, `dashboard.html`, `_products.html`); #1147 only references the unused coach "blocked notifications" msgid.
* **Why here.** Repeat attendance depends on the promised call; the roster and dashboard gaps mean nobody can see whether it happens.

#### Bold

##### CO-B1. A member-visible coach: who is mine, what do they do, how do I reach them (CO-14, CO-24, CO-07, CO-08)
* **User problem.** `/crush-coach/` is a recruitment page ("Become a Crush Coach"), so a first-timer searching "Crush Coach" lands on a job ad and still does not know what *their* coach does; the page contradicts itself ("Verify at Events" vs "Fully remote (events are optional)") and offers two named people's phone numbers as the only way in (CO-24, unverified, `crush_coach.html:201`). Onboarding says a coach "is your point of contact if anything feels off" (`onboarding/coach_intro.html:20-33`) but no coach exists until the first attendance (`signals.py:4199`), and the contact on the dashboard card is a `mailto:` (CO-07, `dashboard.html:353`).
* **Proposed design.** A member explainer ("what your coach does", what is checked at the door, how to reach them) and an in-app contact path instead of `mailto:`; the dashboard card falls back to `profile.assigned_coach`. Verifier: do not write "about a minute" or "nothing to prepare except your ticket QR and a clear photo" until the ID question is answered.
* **Files.** new member page, `views.py`, `dashboard.html`, `partials/_products.html` (#1163 files), `onboarding/coach_intro.html` (use `<h2>` instead of `<label>` for "A few of our coaches"), `crush_coach.html`, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M-L. Needs the product-model decision (what `assigned_coach` means to the member) and the owner's answer to "is photo ID ever asked at the door?"; exposing coach contact is a staffing/privacy call.
* **Metric.** needs data: onboarding drop-off at the coach-intro step; support contacts that ask "who is my coach?".
* **Dependencies.** #1163 (`_products.html`, `dashboard.html`, `views.py`): sequence after.
* **Why bold.** It gives the product's central human promise a face and a channel.

##### CO-B2. A door check-in that keeps working without signal (CO-18, CO-19; unverified)
* **User problem.** An offline tap raises a blocking `alert("Network error")` (`coach.js:1608, 1667, 1722, 1772, 1812`); "Network error or invalid QR code." covers both a dead network and a bad QR (`coach.js:1557`); nothing is queued, so the member must re-present the QR; the header chip "Live/Offline" reflects only the WebSocket; scan-result messages are raw English f-strings with no next step (`views_checkin.py:360-529`).
* **Proposed design.** Cheap part first: toasts instead of alerts, an honest chip ("Live sync off — check-ins still save"), translated and actionable scan messages ("verify this person first (Verify button), then scan again"). Then queue check-ins locally with idempotent retry (a repeat scan is already safe), show "not saved yet" per row, cache the roster for the night.
* **Files.** `coach.js`, `coach_event_checkin.html`, `views_checkin.py`, service worker, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** L (the cheap part is S-M). Door-night behaviour must be tested with real flaky connections.
* **Metric.** needs data: error rates on `POST /api/events/checkin/` per door night; venue connectivity.
* **Dependencies.** None; #1167 covers `confirm()` leftovers on the same page (not this).
* **Why bold.** The door is the single point where every guest's evening starts.

---

### 5. Cross-cutting journey

#### Quick wins

##### XC-QW1. The "check your inbox" page stops saying a mail was sent when it was not (XC-09)  `[P4-1]`
* **User problem.** `/accounts/confirm-email/` for an empty session (reached by "Request a New Link" on an expired link) says "We've sent a verification link to your email address. Click the link in the email to confirm your account." although nothing was sent; no validity period, no human to ask (`account/verification_sent_crush.html:35-39`). Verifier: the sentence is true on the login-before-verify path, so the replacement must be true on both paths.
* **Design.** Replace one paragraph on the page and add one support line under the resend form.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER (verifier fix).**
  * EN: `We've sent a verification link to your email address. Click the link in the email to confirm your account.` -> `Check your inbox for the link we sent. Didn't get it, or has it expired? Enter your email below and we'll send a new one.` + under the form `Still nothing after a few minutes? Write to support@crush.lu.`
  * DE: -> `Schau in dein Postfach nach dem Link, den wir dir geschickt haben. Nichts bekommen oder abgelaufen? Gib unten deine E-Mail-Adresse ein, dann senden wir dir einen neuen Link.` + `Nach ein paar Minuten noch nichts? Schreib an support@crush.lu.`
  * FR: -> `Consultez votre boîte de réception pour retrouver le lien que nous vous avons envoyé. Rien reçu, ou le lien a expiré ? Saisissez votre adresse e-mail ci-dessous et nous vous en enverrons un nouveau.` + `Toujours rien après quelques minutes ? Écrivez à support@crush.lu.`
* **Files.** `account/verification_sent_crush.html`, `.po`. Left for a follow-up: the reset mail/page say "a few hours" while `PASSWORD_RESET_TIMEOUT` is 72 h (`password_reset_key_message.txt:9`, `password_reset_done.html:32`); "3 days" would be hard-coded and drift if the setting changes, so tie it to the setting.
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. Text only: no allauth adapter, view or URL change.
* **Metric.** needs data: "Request a New Link"/"Resend" clicks that end without a verified email within 24 h.
* **Dependencies.** None. The page's language problem (XC-06) stays open.
* **Why here.** First: the gate in front of the whole product.

##### XC-QW2. The nav label no longer says "Not Submitted" after the longest form on the site (XC-07)  `[P4-1]`
* **User problem.** Members who finish the current wizard have `verification_status='pending'` but no `ProfileSubmission`, so the account dropdown and mobile drawer show "Profile Status: Not Submitted" on every page (`base.html:703-708`, `partials/mobile_drawer.html:67-70`; reproduced in EN/DE/FR; door-rejected members without a submission show it too).
* **Design.** Add two template branches keyed on `profile_completion_status` so pending and rejected members get a true label.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.** EN `Profile Status: Not Submitted` -> `Profile Status: Awaiting verification`; DE `Profilstatus: Nicht eingereicht` -> `Profilstatus: Warten auf Verifizierung`; FR `Statut du profil : Non soumis` -> `Statut du profil : En attente de vérification` (existing msgid `Awaiting verification`, no `.po` change). Rejected without submission -> existing `Rejected`.
* **Files.** `base.html`, `partials/mobile_drawer.html` (`profile_completion_status` is already in the context: no processor change).
* **Effort / risk.** S / low. "Approved" vs "verified" (XC-14) is a separate vocabulary pass.
* **Metric.** needs data: support questions asking whether a profile was submitted.
* **Dependencies.** None (`base.html`, `mobile_drawer.html` are not on #1163's list).
* **Why here.** Second: wrong on every page, for every pending member, at peak anxiety.

##### XC-QW3. The password-reset flow in German and French (XC-16)  `[P4-2]`
* **User problem.** The "email sent" page is headed `Auf Updates prüfen` (DE) / `Vérifier les mises à jour` (FR) = "Check for updates"; the reset field label is DE `E-Mail-Adresse *` / FR `Ajouter une adresse e-mail` (= "Add an email address"); the new-password page says `Choisis un nouveau mot de passe` (informal) in a vous locale. The body text is correct, so this is a confidence problem, not a dead end. The same wrong strings ship for `Check Your Email:` on `invitation_pending_approval.html`.
* **Design.** Correct four msgstr (and the same wrong pair on a second page); no template change.
* **BEFORE -> AFTER.** `Check your email`: DE `Auf Updates prüfen` -> `Prüfe deine E-Mails`; FR `Vérifier les mises à jour` -> `Consultez votre boîte mail` (both already used in the page `<title>`). `Email address`: DE `E-Mail-Adresse *` -> `E-Mail-Adresse`; FR `Ajouter une adresse e-mail` -> `Adresse e-mail`. `Choose a new password`: FR `Choisis un nouveau mot de passe` -> `Choisissez un nouveau mot de passe` (page title too).
* **Files.** `.po` only (msgids used by one template each).
* **Effort / risk.** S / none. Not covered by #1154 (shipped-wrong, not fuzzy). Sub-point "Courriel" on the signup/login labels is a Polish inconsistency (Django's own FR catalogue wins).
* **Metric.** needs data: password-reset starts that do not end in a new password within an hour, by language.
* **Dependencies.** None; #1166 owns the FR informal-address count (different strings).
* **Why here.** Third: the recovery path for every member who forgets a password.

#### Medium

##### XC-M1. A translation-completeness gate (S-01; related #1154)
* **User problem.** Strings used by shipped code with no `.po` entry at all show English on DE/FR pages: the curated-application confirmation heading (reproduced), the post-cancel credit flash, "Unable to initiate payment...", the Connect Week card button, the footer link "Cookie Settings" on every page, the credit-expiry reminder email (4 msgids), the rejected-page delete help text, ~10 coach-door strings (CO-20). #1154 tracks fuzzy entries (366 FR / 367 DE) but not missing or empty ones. My regex scan lists about 30 member-facing msgids with no entry in either language after normalising `%%` (a few flagged strings are false positives, e.g. msgids containing escaped quotes; every msgid I cite in this document was confirmed by hand against the `.po` files).
* **Design.** A script (run locally and in CI) that extracts msgids from member-facing templates and `_()` calls and fails when a msgid touched in the PR is missing, empty or fuzzy in DE/FR; extend `manage.py check_translations` accordingly. CI wiring is a workflow change: **propose only**.
* **Files.** a new script (or an extension of `manage.py check_translations`) that scans member-facing templates and `_()` calls, a test that runs it for touched msgids, and the CI wiring (workflow edit: propose only).
* **Effort / risk.** M. `.github/workflows` must not be edited here.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of DE/FR sessions; English strings per DE/FR page.
* **Dependencies.** #1154, #1166 (recap.html empties).
* **Why here.** One missing step produced five findings; a gate stops the next ones.

##### XC-M2. Money a member holds is visible and safe (XC-08, XC-02, SD-10)
* **User problem.** No template shows a Crush Credit balance or expiry; the balance is read only to decide whether "Pay with Crush Credit" appears (`views_events.py:833-851`), a EUR 4.50 remainder after an organiser-cancellation credit can never satisfy it, the expiry-reminder command has no timer. Deleting a Crush.lu profile voids all credit and deletes paid seats without issuing cancellation credit, and the confirm page says only "Cancels any pending event registrations" (`delete_crushlu_profile_confirm.html:21`; reproduced: balance 1550 cents -> 0, registration deleted).
* **Design.** A "Crush Credit: EUR X — valid until ..." line on My Events and the account page with per-credit dates (never "6 months" as a blanket); in the deletion confirm, a dynamic block shown only for credit that will actually be voided (verifier: an unspent cash-refund-eligible organiser-cancellation credit survives; "cancel before {deadline} to receive credit" is true only before the late window); the bullet becomes "Cancels your event registrations (paid seats are not credited)". Partial use ("pay the rest by card") would be false today: redemption is all-or-nothing, so any such copy waits for the product decision on credit + card top-up. Ops, propose only: put the expiry-reminder sweep on the timer.
* **Files.** `views_events.py`, `my_events.html`, account templates, `views_account.py`, `delete_crushlu_profile_confirm.html`, `gdpr_data_management.html:84`.
* **Effort / risk.** M. Untested on Postgres (lock order); legal wording.
* **Metric.** needs data: members with an unexpired positive balance; credit expiring unspent per month; deleting members with credit or a paid upcoming seat; whether ops runs `send_crush_credit_expiry_reminders`.
* **Dependencies.** `dashboard.html` is #1163's: put the balance on My Events/account.
* **Why here.** Credit is the only remedy the cancellation policy offers; invisible credit reads as lost money.

##### XC-M3. One vocabulary for "verified" and one honest answer to "who sees my photo?" (XC-14, CC-14, XC-12, XC-13, CO-14)
* **User problem.** The dashboard says "You're verified" and "Get verified to join Crush Connect" together (CC-14); the door push says "Your profile is approved ... You can now register for events" (XC-14); the nav says "Approved" while pending members can already register for entry events. ID is mentioned only in the confirmation and reminder emails (XC-12) while the verification explanations say "that's all it takes". The photo step never says who sees photos (XC-13; the policy answer lives on two pages).
* **Design (owner decisions first).** Two terms (CC-14: "Profile approved" = a coach reviewed it, "Identity confirmed" = LuxID or a coach met you) applied in one pass, including "✓ Verified profile" and "Everyone in Connect is a verified, real person"; a new msgid or `pgettext` for member-facing "Approved" labels (the msgid is shared with coach pages); verifier fixes: the door-push text must keep "and join verified-members events", the LuxID line must not say "other members can find you in Connect Week" for LuxID-only members in the beta. Door: state only what the emails already promise (`Please bring a valid photo ID for age verification`) until ops confirm what coaches check; no invented duration. Photos: reuse the vetted sentence `Your photos are never public or shown to search engines — you need a Crush.lu account to see them, and Crush Connect asks before showing your photo to your matches.`; a longer "only X, Y, Z" list needs the privacy owner (members see only photo_1, only for approved, unblocked pairs, within the connection window or an EventConnection, or a Connect pairing with consent).
* **Files.** `_verification_journey.html`, `profile_submitted.html`, `membership.html` (all #1163), `_connect_status_strip.html`, `notification_service.py`, `push_notifications.py`, `edit_profile.html`, `create_profile.html`, `event_detail.html`, emails, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** M-L. Large surface, owner/legal decisions.
* **Metric.** needs data: what coaches verify at the door (ID, photo match or both) and door turn-aways for missing ID; wizard step-3 abandonment and photo-less submissions; onboarding drop-off at the coach-intro step.
* **Dependencies.** **#1163** (three of the main surfaces); #1166 (ungated LuxID claims), #1054 (stale "blur" copy).
* **Why here.** First-timer anxiety is the top-of-funnel blocker, but most of the fix waits on decisions and on #1163.

#### Bold

##### XC-B1. A signup that lands you in the journey, in your language (XC-06, XC-15, XC-21, XC-22)
* **User problem.** A French signup on an English-default phone gets a French mail, then an English waiting page, English login and English emails: the language-neutral URLs follow the browser, the profile is created at first login and the "sync" signal is a no-op (`azureproject/adapters.py:1096`, `signals.py:498-510`; reproduced). After verifying, the member re-types a password and lands on the dashboard "Step 1 of 4", skipping the welcome page and phone step (`adapters.py:972-977`; XC-15, verifier: the "4" is the create-profile wizard's own count). Four time promises coexist (15, 12, 5-10 and 5 minutes; XC-21, unverified). The verification mail greets a lower-cased generated username (XC-22, unverified).
* **Proposed design.** Set the language cookie on signup and carry a signed `?l=` through the confirmation URL; store the language at signup; send first logins to the guided onboarding entry gated on `verification_status == 'incomplete'` (verifier: gating on `get_current_step < 4` would push legacy verified members through `/welcome/`); derive one time number from `onboarding.JOURNEY_STEPS` and measure the median before quoting it; greet by first name.
* **Files.** `views_account.py` (signup), `azureproject/adapters.py`, `signals.py`, `account/email/*`, `auth.html`, `welcome.html`, `emails/welcome.html`, `_verification_journey.html` (#1163: the step counter).
* **Effort / risk.** M. Auth adapter and signup view: **propose only**; legacy-member regression risk.
* **Metric.** needs data: share of signups whose `Accept-Language` differs from the `/xx/` path language (not logged today); funnel from first login to `phone_verified` and to submitted; median minutes from first `/welcome/` to submitted profile.
* **Dependencies.** #1163 (`_verification_journey.html`, `dashboard.html`).
* **Why bold.** It fixes the first impression for exactly the members the trilingual promise is meant for.

##### XC-B2. One notification system for event and Connect lifecycle (SD-18, CC-10, XC-18, XC-03, XC-04, XC-05, CO-09)
* **User problem.** Channels are wired by hand per feature. Waitlist promotion, curated invitation and payment confirmation are email only (SD-18); "It's mutual!" is a bell only (CC-10); every chat message yields push + email + bell (XC-18, `notification_service.py:5-8, 226-262`); payment-pending, waitlist and cancellation mails ignore preferences while the registration confirmation obeys "Event reminders" (`email_helpers.py:605`), so a member who pauses reminders silently loses the confirmation of a paid seat, and "Unsubscribe from ALL Emails — You won't receive any emails" is untrue both ways (XC-05, `email_unsubscribe.html:171`); the "Marketing & Promotions" toggle gates no send and newsletters follow an `email_newsletter` flag with no toggle (XC-03); the WhatsApp toggle promises chat alerts and event reminders that are never sent on WhatsApp while authorising campaign WhatsApp (XC-04, `edit_account_notifications.html:94`); the coach's My Crush! reminder rides a "Screening Reminders" flag (CO-09).
* **Proposed design.** A per-event-type channel policy (transactional vs reminder vs marketing), bell text in the recipient's language everywhere, push for promotion/invitation/accepted, a chat digest, and a preference page that says what each switch does. Verifier warnings: rewiring the Marketing toggle changes who receives campaigns; XC-05 copy is only true after the gate at `email_helpers.py:605` changes; the WhatsApp stop-gap copy must not promise env-gated sends; the push half of XC-04 was not supported (campaign push to every subscription matches the prompt wording).
* **Files.** `notification_service.py`, `email_helpers.py` (#1163 file), `push_notifications.py`, `services/campaigns.py`, `edit_account_notifications.html`, `email_unsubscribe.html`, `models/profiles.py`, migrations, `.po`.
* **Effort / risk.** L. Consent semantics need owner and legal approval; model change.
* **Metric.** needs data: members with `whatsapp_opt_in` who received a campaign WhatsApp; messages per thread per day and emails per member per week; "Pause all emails" rate after a first chat; delivery/open rate of the payment-pending mail.
* **Dependencies.** #1163 (`email_helpers.py`), #1147 (Android notification settings link), #1166/#1167 (adjacent).
* **Why bold.** It replaces a dozen one-off decisions that already disagree with each other.

## 5. Fix PRs opened from this review

All four are **drafts** against `main`, copy/template-only (no model, migration, payment, auth or permission change), with EN/DE/FR updated together and `.mo` files rebuilt with polib (never committed). Each edits the two `django.po` files in place, away from end-of-file, because #1163 and #1175 also edit them there. Each PR has a ready test file with literal paths and negative controls; I re-ran every new test file myself after the branches were pushed. Full BEFORE/AFTER tables, test commands, deviations and review items are in each PR description.

| # | PR | Findings | Result of my independent run |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | [#1192](https://github.com/EuphoriaLux/Multi-Domain-Django-Platform/pull/1192) CX: stop screens from saying the wrong thing (waitlist pay button, "Not Submitted", verification link, Connect numbers) | SD-06, XC-07, XC-09, SD-11, CC-20, CC-06, CC-15 (+ two SD-20 fragments) | 28 passed, 37 subtests |
| 2 | [#1197](https://github.com/EuphoriaLux/Multi-Domain-Django-Platform/pull/1197) CX: fix wrong and missing DE/FR strings on register, ticket, cancel, reset and curated-application screens | QN-18, XC-16, SD-14, SD-02 (translation slice), plus a catalogue scan (S-01) reproduced by rendering | 35 passed, 35 subtests |
| 3 | [#1199](https://github.com/EuphoriaLux/Multi-Domain-Django-Platform/pull/1199) CX: fix four event/profile emails (recap, waitlist, quiz night, rejection) | SD-08, XC-10, QN-15, CO-03 (+ one DE typo) | 21 passed, 67 subtests |
| 4 | [#1200](https://github.com/EuphoriaLux/Multi-Domain-Django-Platform/pull/1200) CX: translate the Connect consent, card button, lobby safety path and My Crush! flow (DE/FR) | CC-02, CC-18, SD-04 (widened) | 36 passed |

**PR 1 (#1192, `claude/cx-fix-1-untrue-screens`):** Waitlisted members no longer get a Pay button the server refuses (display condition only; pending and confirmed-but-unpaid still show it, asserted). Pending and rejected members see "Awaiting verification" / "Rejected" instead of "Not Submitted". Expired-link page no longer claims a mail was sent. Connect says up to 2 daily profiles (not 3) and no longer promises a coach arranging free-member dates. Weekly review labels the Yes/No as the member's own guess. 16 files, 28 new tests (37 subtests); I re-ran them: 28 passed. Follow-up (c276f8b): three automated-review (Codex, P2) comments were verified against the code and fixed (the waitlist sentence is hidden when a payment is already held; the no-session verification line no longer says "the link we sent"; the daily count reads "up to" two); the owner had already merged `main` into the branch, and CI was green on the first head.

**PR 2 (#1197, `claude/cx-fix-2-de-fr-flows`):** .po edits plus one gettext string in views_events.py. FR "Back to Event" no longer reads "Retour à la connexion"; the DE/FR password-reset heading no longer says "Check for updates"; cancel refusals and the curated-application card, banner and flash now exist in DE/FR; the "already started" refusal points to support@crush.lu instead of "contact your coach". The scan's "Cookie Settings" claim did not reproduce and was dropped. 35 new tests (35 subtests); I re-ran them: 35 passed.

**PR 3 (#1199, `claude/cx-fix-3-event-emails`):** Recap email says until when the connection window is open instead of "the next event is around the corner" (no coach-call promise added). Paid-event waitlist email says the promoted member must pay to confirm. Quiz-night confirmation and reminder tell guests to bring a charged phone and what to tap after check-in. Rejection email no longer suggests creating a new profile. email_helpers.py untouched. 21 new tests (67 subtests); I re-ran them: 21 passed.

**PR 4 (#1200, `claude/cx-fix-4-connect-de-fr`):** Bulk DE/FR translation: 56 entries filled or replaced (28 were fuzzy with wrong text), 3 new, plus the Connect Week button "Answer" -> "Save my guesses". Consent checkboxes are faithful translations of the current English. Translates, but does not verify, promises such as "your coach will call you within 48 hours". **Must not merge before native DE/FR and owner review.** 36 new tests; I re-ran them: 36 passed.

`P4-1`…`P4-4` in this report are PRs 1…4 above (#1192, #1197, #1199, #1200). `S-01` is a catalogue scan item found while building the shortlist, not one of the 123 findings; it was reproduced by rendering inside PR 2 (and its "Cookie Settings" part did not reproduce and was dropped).

Honest limits: the agents' pytest runs used `-o addopts=""` because `pytest-xdist` is not installed in this sandbox's venv (so `pytest.ini`'s `-n auto` is rejected); CI runs the real configuration and the full suite, which no agent ran. DE/FR strings were written by an AI and need native-speaker review. Anything that promises a human action or deadline was kept out unless it already ships in English.

## 6. Questions that need data or an owner answer


Repo HEAD `ec37261`, 2026-10-05. This repo has no production data, analytics, vault or secrets, so every claim about "how often" is open. Rows are merged across findings; "Decision it changes" says what the team would do differently depending on the answer. "Where" is a suggestion (DB query, access log, App Insights, App Service settings, or a person), not something I ran.

Priority tags: **A** = one-line answer, changes a recommendation immediately; **B** = needs a query or log; **C** = owner/policy decision rather than data.
