## 7. Areas skipped and known limits

Per-product notes from the reviewers (what they could not exercise, and why). Items under "Local data I created" refer to throw-away rows in the reviewers' local SQLite database and are omitted here.

### 7.1 Speed Dating


#### Could not exercise in this sandbox
- **SumUp card widget, 3-D Secure, webhook, status polling, declined/expired checkouts.** `SUMUP_API_KEY` is not configured locally, so `create_sumup_event_checkout` returns a generic 500 JSON. I read `views_payments.py`, `sumup_widget.html`, `core.js` (`sumupCheckoutButton`) and the return handler instead; the "payment failed / expired" copy quoted in the journey comes from code, not from a rendered failure. Capture-time remedies (curated group no longer certified, member cancelled while paying) were traced in `_apply_paid_checkout`; the on-site state after the curated remedy (SD-12) was reproduced by calling `credit_registration_for_unavailable_curated_group` directly.
- **Anything that depends on `select_for_update` / lock order (SQLite ignores it): SD-01 (checkout-claim lifecycle), SD-05 (hold semantics under concurrent waitlist promotion), SD-12 (capture-time remedy), SD-16 (notice claiming).** Conclusions there are code-reading only and are untested on Postgres.
- **Mobile layout, tap targets, keyboard overlap, contrast, focus order (checklist 9 and 10).** Playwright's browser build in `/opt/pw-browsers` (chromium-1194) does not match the venv's Playwright (expects 1243); I launched it by `executable_path` and loaded three pages at 390 px, but Tailwind/static CSS did not load in this sandbox (pages rendered unstyled), so any layout, tap-target or contrast observation would have been meaningless and none is reported. Accessibility was reviewed from templates only: sr-only checkbox tiles with `peer-focus-visible` and fieldset/legend on the preference form (good), `aria-live` on the HTMX form/success containers, icon-only My Events links have `aria-label` (good; SD-22 notes the wording). No screen-reader run was done.
- **Real reminder/recap/feedback timers.** I did not run the management commands against the DB (they would stamp `reminder_sent_at` etc. on shared rows); I called the email helpers with a locmem outbox (no real mail was sent, nothing was written under the repo) and read command/timer schedules from `function_app.py`. Web-push and native-push delivery were not testable (no subscriptions); push text and the UTC bug (SD-03) come from code plus a shell computation.
- **Apple/Google Wallet pass rendering** (no certificates): only the field construction code was read.
- **Anonymous event detail** was read in the template (login/sign-up CTA branches) but not rendered; the anonymous speed-dating guide was rendered in EN/DE/FR.

#### Not reviewed (out of scope or owned elsewhere)
- Coach panel, coach check-in scanner UI, printed door badge (`ticket_printer.py`) beyond noting it also prints the "PHASE 1 // TONIGHT'S ACTIVITY VOTING" block when voting is enabled; #1176 owns coach roster counts.
- Dashboard next-event card and the Premium/choose-coach surfaces (#1163, #925/#1164, #1099 item 1).
- Crush Connect Event Lobby recap (`recap.html`, #1166), Premium reserved-seat banner, Quiz Night, Crush Cache, presentations/voting internals beyond what a speed-dating member sees.
- Newsletter/campaign WhatsApp and push (a different product); I confirmed by grep that no event-lifecycle message in the speed-dating flow uses WhatsApp.
- Gender-capped direct events (pool chips, "your gender group is full") - seeded events 12/22 have no caps and curated events ignore them, so those states were read (`registration_waitlist_warning.html`) but not rendered.
- Free curated event (id 21), free direct events, private/invitation events, plus-one/dietary fields (not shown on speed-dating events), re-registration after cancellation (read: `registered_at` reset, back of the waitlist).
- Rate-limit 429 page for `event_register` (5/h per user) - not exercised; needs data on real 429 frequency.
- Photos/real event testimonials (#1055), privacy-policy "blur photos" copy (#1054).

#### Languages / personas
- EN fully rendered for all states. DE and FR rendered for: curated event page (pending + locked group), speed-dating guide, post-event attendees page, invitation/recap/feedback/reminder emails (DE/FR), curated reserve email (FR). Waitlist, cancel, ticket and payment pages in DE/FR were checked through `.po` (msgstr empty/fuzzy/tone via polib) rather than rendered.
- Persona 3 "screen reader" and "slow connection" are covered only by code reading (loading states exist: "Preparing checkout...", "Processing...", SumUp loader; offline ticket is covered by existing tests, not re-run).
- Luxembourgish: seeded events list `lu` as an event language; the UI itself has no LU translation (not reported).

#### Seed-data artefacts deliberately not reported
- Local events 20-22 have English-only title/description, so DE/FR pages show English marketing text; the September command ships `description_de/_fr`, so this is not a production defect.
- All 12 seeded `testuser*` accounts have empty `event_languages`, so they hit the "Language Requirement" block on every event with languages; the profile wizard lists `event_languages` as a required step for real members (`models/profiles.py:1015, 1053-1056`), so this is a seed artefact.
- Event times render as 23:xx etc. because the seed uses "now + N days" in UTC.
- Absolute links in test-client emails use http://crush.lu (request scheme); production sits behind HTTPS.

### 7.2 Quiz Night


#### Environment limits (these cap confidence on the findings that say "read only")
- No real sockets. The dev server is plain WSGI runserver, so all live behaviour was driven with `channels.testing.WebsocketCommunicator`
  against the local SQLite DB (throw-away scripts, not committed: `qn_ws2.py`, `qn_ws3.py`, `qn_ws4.py`). `daphne` is not installed, so the scripts stub
  `daphne.testing` in `sys.modules` (no repo change). The channel layer was the in-memory one; production Redis fan-out, multi-worker broadcast and
  real network drops were not exercised.
- Static assets 404 on the sandbox dev server (CSS/JS served as text/html), so Chromium could not run `quiz-live.js` or show the real styling.
  Consequence: NO visual check, no tap-target measurement, no on-device keyboard/one-handed check, no real screen-reader run. Anything about layout,
  contrast and tap targets is from reading markup/CSS (contrast in QN-24 is a numeric estimate from Tailwind v4 token values: re-run axe).
- SQLite ignores `select_for_update`. Findings QN-02, QN-07, QN-08 touch paths that take row locks (`assign_table_on_checkin`, `generate_rotation_rounds`,
  `score_table_for_question`, `advance_round_and_rotate`): their logic was reproduced, their lock ordering/concurrency is UNTESTED on Postgres.
- pytest was not run (run once, by the person coordinating the review). Tests were read for stated intent: `test_quiz.py` (TestReScoreBeforeReveal, TestResetQuiz, TestQuizTableDisplay,
  dissolve/consolidation classes), `test_quiz_consolidation.py`, `test_quiz_packs.py`, `test_event_format_guides.py`, `test_ux_wave5_a11y_contrast.py`.

#### Brief items that do not exist in this product (not a gap in the review)
- Join code, player URL with a code, team naming, nickname: none. A guest "joins" by being checked in (coach scan) and opening the same attendee URL; the
  projector QR is only a shortcut to that URL (`views_quiz.py:358-372`). The "team" is a table number; the only identity on screen is `display_name`.
- Phone answer entry / per-player answer feedback: the legacy `table_answer` path exists but is rejected for quiz nights
  (`consumers.py:467-472` "Individual answers are not used in quiz night events."), so there is no player answer UI to review.
- Push, WhatsApp or SMS touchpoints for Quiz Night: none found (grep over push/whatsapp/notification services and templates). Only email.
- A quiz-specific certificate or points award: none found (nothing outside the quiz modules reads IndividualScore/TableRoundScore). The certificate-page
  overflow (#1168) and points plural issues (#1154) are on the do-not-touch list and were not re-reported.

#### Areas read but not rendered / only partly covered
- Host pages were read, not rendered, in DE/FR (`quiz_coach.html`, `coach_quiz_config.html`, question/round forms): their DE/FR copy was checked through
  the .po files (polib), none of the quiz strings were `fuzzy` or empty. Rendered: EN/DE/FR player shell (`/<lang>/events/23/quiz/`) with its `data-i18n`,
  EN/DE/FR projector page (`/<lang>/quiz/23/display/`), EN/DE event page and registered/attended/live/ended states, FR/DE register page, EN guide strings via .po,
  coach event page and 404 gates as coach.sophie / admin_cx.
- Emails (registration confirmation, reminder, recap, feedback request) were read as templates + .po, not rendered or sent.
- Luxembourgish: event 13 is configured for "de, lu"; quiz UI only supports en/de/fr, so nothing was checked for lb.
- Registration and payment for quiz nights (event_register, SumUp checkout, cancellation/refund rules) are the shared flow reviewed under the sd product; I only
  rendered the register page for a paid qn_ quiz event (fee, no refund/deadline text on the page) and did not file it here.
- Door check-in (coach scanner UI, badge printing `services/ticket_printer.py`, manual assign, consolidate preview UI) were reviewed only where they feed the quiz
  (assignment, undo, broadcasts).
- Media stimuli (`quiz-media.js`: image/video/audio/iframe embeds, autoplay policy on phones, caption support): not exercised; only the authoring a11y
  description field and the `media` payload shape were read.
- Question content quality/tone of the packs (`crush_lu/quiz_packs/*.py`) was not reviewed; only translation completeness was checked in the DB.
- Quiz packs, `hub/` CRM, `CheckinConsumer` and `CacheHuntConsumer` (same file, other products) were not reviewed.

#### Considered and not filed (kept under the 25-finding cap or out of CX scope)
- Projector date line uses a fixed `date:'D, M j — g:i A'` format (`quiz_display.html:19`), 12-hour/English-style order for DE/FR (minor).
- `display_name` falls back to `username.split("@")[0]` when first_name is empty (`models/profiles.py:909-912`), which could put an email local part on the projector.
- "Teams of 4 / 2+2" and `max_participants_m/f/nb` event caps interact with the quiz roles; only the guest-facing copy contradiction was filed (QN-14).
- Security-adjacent observations, flagged for the review coordinator, NOT CX findings and not proposed as edits: (1) the `quiz.rotate` broadcast carries every attendee's internal
  user id, first name, table and role to every phone (`consumers.py:1865-1893`) although `quiz_table_update` deliberately omits internal ids (`consumers.py:933-935`,
  AUTHZ-02); (2) `GET /api/quiz/<id>/state/` and `/my-assignment/` require only a login, not a registration (`api_quiz.py:51-60, 188`), so any logged-in member can read
  the current question text of any quiz id; (3) the page gate (`attended`) and the socket gate (`confirmed` or `attended`) differ (`views_quiz.py:193-198` vs `consumers.py:1107-1111`).
  No permission logic was changed or proposed beyond the denial copy in QN-06/QN-20.

### 7.3 Crush Connect


#### Conclusions that are UNTESTED ON POSTGRES (SQLite ignores `select_for_update`)
- CC-03 (paused recipient, request expiry) and CC-07 (accept/decline/expire outcomes) depend on `sync_request_state`, `respond_to_weekly_request` and `send_weekly_request` (`services/connect_cycle.py:649-847`), which lock rows. I read the code and rendered the states on SQLite; I make no claim about concurrent behaviour (double accept, accept racing expiry).
- Chat sends and venue plans (`services/connect_chat.py`, `sync_chat_state` with `select_for_update`) were rendered once, not raced.
- Premium confirm/cancel lock ordering (`PremiumMembership` before `CrushProfile`, `models/profiles.py:2967-3070`) was read only; payment paths are out of scope and no change is proposed there beyond copy and a product decision (CC-01).

#### Not reproduced or not verified
- **LuxID connect flow.** No LuxID `SocialApp` is configured locally, so `luxid_connect_url` is `None` and the `Connect LuxID` button never rendered (CC-05, CC-14). I assumed it appears on prod from the template logic. Link, callback and error states are unreviewed.
- **Prod phase flags.** `CRUSH_CONNECT_CANDIDATE_OPEN`, `CRUSH_CONNECT_LAUNCHED`, `CRUSH_EVENT_LOBBY_ENABLED`, `PREMIUM_REDIRECTS_TO_BETA` are unknown. I rendered BETA (candidate open) and LIVE and set `CRUSH_EVENT_LOBBY_ENABLED=true` for the lobby pages. PRELAUNCH was not rendered (everyone non-staff is bounced to the teaser, same as CC-05).
- **Event Lobby live behaviour.** WebSocket/Redis, signal sending, mutual reveal, countdown and the 15s poll were not exercised; I only read server-rendered shells and templates. JS-rendered strings (tile labels, banners) were not captured by the text renderer.
- **Real photos.** All seeded photos reuse one file; photo serving rules (`views_media.can_view_profile_photo`), blur/hide-photo states and the removal flow's side effects were read, not exercised.
- **Email and push delivery.** Nothing was sent. Email templates and notification code were read; rendered copy in each language was checked only for `connect_beta_invite` and `connect_week_request` via the `.po`.
- **Screen readers, dark mode, slow connection.** Not tested. Accessibility findings (CC-21) are from markup; the only runtime check was Chromium 390x844 tap-target and position measurements on the chat and Connect Week pages (the dev server returns 404 for `/static/`, so my script served files from `crush_lu/static` by request interception).
- **DE/FR rendering.** Rendered in DE and FR: Connect Week home, onboarding step 7, People I've Met profile, Event Lobby locked page. Everything else in DE/FR was checked through a `polib` audit of every msgid used by the Connect templates, forms, views and services (710 msgids scanned; 49 flagged per language, a handful of which are matching artefacts such as email strings written with `{{ var }}` placeholders, which I checked by hand and excluded from the findings). I did not read all shipped DE/FR msgstr for tone; the FR informal-address count belongs to #1166.
- **Wizard validation errors.** Only step 7 was driven to an error state. Steps 1-6 error handling, back-navigation and resume after a half-filled step were not exercised.
- **Coach-side screens** (`coach_connect_members`, `coach_connect_member`, `coach_connections`) were skimmed only to see what a coach can do with an accepted pick; the Coach Panel campaigns are out of scope.
- **iOS-app suppression** (`premium/ios_unavailable.html`, `ios_commerce_suppressed`) not rendered.
- **Quiz Night and Speed Dating**: other reviewers.

#### Deliberately not re-reported (cited instead)
#1163, #925/#1164 (captured Premium payment not applied; `choose_coach` pending/Pay), #1165 (axe follow-ups, timeline contrast, confirm-sheet fallback), #1166 (ungated LuxID claims; recap.html empties; FR "Salut"), #1167 (contact-choices scope, `confirm()` leftovers, `Contact Info Shared` label), #1154 (fuzzy backlog), #1054 (blur-photo wording), #1147, #1055. Where my findings touch a file in #1163's list (`_products.html`, `_verification_journey.html`, `choose_coach.html`, `membership.html`, `dashboard.html`) I flagged "apply after #1163" instead of proposing parallel edits.

#### Parking lot: suspected but not verified (low confidence, not filed)
1. **Two pause mechanisms.** Profile-level "Take a break" mirrors a Connect pause (`models/profiles.py:1480-1517`, `paused_by_break`). The Connect hub paused card does not mention a break, and `crush_connect_reactivate` clears only the Connect side, leaving `on_break_at` set (event invitations and marketing stay suppressed). Not rendered end to end.
2. **Excluded-by-coach members** are bounced silently to the teaser and then see "Identity verified — you can join Crush Connect" plus the waitlist (`views_static.py:230-252`). This is deliberate privacy-by-design, but it is a misleading state with no way to ask why; needs a product/legal decision.
3. **Block from a Connect Week card** is a single tap with no confirm sheet (the chat block has one, `moderation/_block_report_menu.html:57-61`), and unblocking via Blocked members does not obviously lift a Connect `ConnectPairExclusion`. Not verified.
4. **"People I've Met" profile shows `story_answer`** (`person_profile.html:34-37`) although the Story step was replaced by the 3 questions; likely legacy data. Not checked against prod.
5. **Naming overlap**: "My Crush!", "My Matches", the "Matches" tab, "People I've Met", "Connect", "the Mix". Worth a vocabulary pass; not filed as a finding because I could not rank it against the rest without usage data (`needs data: tap-through from the Matches tab to each of its entries`).
6. **Alpine strings**: `connect.js:17` hardcodes `"Any age"`; `window.confirm` at `connect.js:524` is covered by #1167.
7. **Hub "Today" tab for LuxID-only members** points at the catalogue status page, which is labelled "You're in the mix", not "Today". Minor.

### 7.4 Crush Coach and Coach Panel


#### Do-not-touch items respected (cited, not re-reported)
- Premium payment recovery, captured-payment-not-applied, `choose_coach` "payment pending" + Pay button: #1163 / #925 / #1164. I only looked at the entry redirect (CO-12) and the legacy `_verification_options` payment-pending card text was read but not reported.
- `coach_event_detail` roster counting: #1176 (CO-04 is about the *scanner* page, which that PR does not touch; CO-25 only reads the curated-groups copy).
- #1166 FR "Salut" greetings (mentioned inside CO-22 only as out-of-scope), LuxID claims on home/how_it_works/crush_connect, recap.html empty DE/FR: not reviewed.
- #1147 Android notification settings link / unused coach "blocked notifications" msgid: referenced from CO-09 only.
- #1167 `confirm()` leftovers: the door page also uses `window.confirm` for Undo and Photo mismatch; I did not report that separately (CO-18 covers the `alert()` calls on failure and says so).
- #1154 fuzzy/plural counts: CO-20 reports *missing* msgids (not fuzzy) on the coach panel, which #1154 does not count.

#### States I could not reproduce, or reproduced only partly
- **Curated applications / group assignment (selecting, confirming):** I could only render an empty curated event (id 20). Creating realistic `applied` registrations with `EventRegistrationPreference`, running "Generate fair curated groups" (admin action) and the payment-due/complete states needs the admin generator and more fixture work than the time allowed. CO-25 is a copy-only observation from reading the panel template; I have no evidence on selection speed, group repair flow, or the member emails around withdrawal/reserve (`curated_group_*` templates were not read).
- **Camera scanning:** Chromium has no camera here, so I could not drive a real QR scan. I exercised the same endpoint through the manual "Check In" buttons (which post to the identical signed URL) and read `handleScan` and `event_checkin_api`. Torch/printer (RawBT) hardware paths were not exercised.
- **WebSocket live sync between two coaches:** the local dev server is plain WSGI (no `USE_ASGI_DEV`), so `/ws/checkin/<id>/` returns 404 and the page shows "Offline" locally. I therefore did not test the broadcast path (`handleRemoteCheckin`, remote undo/reject) — read only.
- **Real offline / slow networks:** tested only an instant offline toggle in Chromium (CO-18). No throttled-3G or flaky-connection run, no service-worker behaviour on the coach pages (SW caches the member ticket; it does not queue coach POSTs — read only).
- **Push notifications (coach and member):** `coach_notifications.py` / `api_coach_push.py` read; no VAPID keys or push service available, so no delivery, language, or lock-screen text was observed. CO-09 is code-based (medium confidence); the real coach opt-in rate is unknown (needs data).
- **WhatsApp touchpoints:** campaign WhatsApp templates and the legacy `wa.me` coach links were read (recontact email, profile_submitted). No Meta calls, no template catalogue; the composer's WhatsApp template list rendered empty locally.
- **Legacy coach review UI (`/coach/review/<id>/`, call logging, SMS invite, pre-screening, verification channel, Sparks, Journeys, quiz config, presentation control, match pairs):** not deep-reviewed. After the 2026-07-11 pivot these are either legacy or other products' surfaces (quiz night = qn, speed dating = sd, Crush Connect = cc). I only confirmed they exist from the dashboard nav and urls.
- **Member<->coach messaging:** there is no in-app channel; the only paths are `mailto:` on the dashboard card (visible only for legacy-submission members, CO-07) and wa.me/tel in legacy states. I did not review `views_connect_chat` (member<->member).
- **Post-event member pages** (`event_attendees`, `request_connection`, recap email, connection review): the attendees page 302s unless the viewer attended an event with other attended members; I did not seed a full attendee set. CO-15/CO-09 quote the templates and the coach task page by reading them, not from a rendered member flow. The coach-side `coach_connection_review` was only rendered in its empty state.
- **Screening/booking emails** (`screening_confirmed`, `screening_fallback_offered`, `pre_screening_invite`, `coach_assignment`): read the existence and triggers; did not render DE/FR. Legacy; reach unknown (needs data: legacy submission counts, see CO-16).
- **Accessibility:** no axe run, no screen-reader pass, no keyboard-focus order check on the door page. Only static observations (a `<label>` used as a heading in coach_intro, hover-only tooltip for "No photo", `alert()` dialogs). Contrast tokens not measured.
- **Phone-width coverage:** Chromium 390x844 only for the check-in page. Other coach pages were rendered as text (no layout check). Tablet/desktop door use not examined.
- **Other languages / formality:** DE coach-panel strings use formal "Sie" in at least one place ("Tippen Sie auf einen Namen, um einen Teilnehmer manuell einzuchecken.") while member copy uses "du". I left this out because I could not tell whether the coach panel is deliberately formal; worth an owner decision.

#### Environment caveats
- **SQLite-only 500:** `views.py::profile_submitted` runs `Extract("review_duration","epoch")` on a DurationField once 5+ submissions have `reviewed_at`; SQLite raises "Extract requires native DurationField database support" (HTTP 500 in the local DB). PostgreSQL supports it. I rendered that page through `working folder/cx_coach.py`, a private copy of a page-render helper that swallows that one aggregate error. Not reported as a product finding. The shared a page-render helper was restored to its original content after a brief accidental edit; nobody else's file was left changed.
- **Static files:** the dev server (DEBUG off) 404s `/static/…`, so for the Chromium run I fulfilled `/static/**` from the Django finders (`coach_shot.py`). Layout measurements (44 px button heights, 4 px gap) come from that run.
- **Playwright version mismatch:** the venv's Playwright expects chromium-1243; only 1194 exists in /opt/pw-browsers, so I launched `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome` directly (no install performed).
- **Untested on Postgres / locking:** door actions (check-in, undo, verify, reject, promote) use `select_for_update` and conditional UPDATE claims (`claim_profile_verification`, `reject_door_verification`). SQLite ignores `select_for_update`, and none of my findings depend on lock ordering or concurrency outcomes, but any statement about double-scans, concurrent coaches, or undo/verify races is read-only reasoning, **untested on Postgres**. CO-05 (verify-before-compare) is a UX-timing finding, not a race finding.
- **Local data created (all prefixed `coach_`):** users `coach_free_pending`, `coach_sub_pending|rev|rej|recontact|fallback|escalated|paused|nocoach`, `coach_rejected_door`, `coach_door_anna|bea|cara|dora|eli|finn|gus|hugo|ivan`; event 24 "coach_ Door Night (speed dating)" with 9 registrations; check-ins done by tapping on Anna/Bea/Cara (Eli's offline tap failed by design). These rows now influence shared counts (e.g. coach dashboard "Awaiting verification", campaign segment counts) in the local SQLite DB. Seed scripts: `coach_seed.py`, `coach_seed2.py`. No repo file was edited; no migrations, flush or pytest were run.
- **No numbers invented:** every "how often / how many in production" question is recorded as `needs data: ...` in the finding it affects (door rejections vs reversals, photo-less check-ins, coach push opt-in, legacy submission counts, CAMPAIGN_DISPATCH_ENABLED in prod, 'Go Premium' taps vs waitlist joins).

### 7.5 Cross-cutting member journey


#### Hard limits of this review
- Local repo and local SQLite only. No production data, analytics, vault or secrets. Every "needs data" line in Appendix A names the metric that would settle the question; I made no usage claims.
- **Untested on Postgres.** SQLite ignores `select_for_update`. Findings whose behaviour touches locked paths (XC-02 deletion/credit void, XC-08 credit, XC-10 waitlist promotion) were verified for *what happens*, not for *lock ordering*. PaymentTransaction -> CrushProfile ordering was not examined.
- I did not run pytest (run once, by the person coordinating the review), did not edit any repo file, and did not commit. The only writes are `xc_*` rows in the local DB (users, profiles, registrations, one credit) and files in the working folder.
- Local `sent_emails/` was not written: emails were captured by stubbing `send_domain_email` / using a locmem backend inside scripts.

#### Areas not covered or only partly covered
| Area | What I did | What I could not do / why |
|---|---|---|
| Social providers incl. LuxID (signup, login, consent pages, `oauth_landing`, `socialaccount/signup_crush.html`) | read templates and signal handlers; rendered the phone step; traced `auto_approve_profile_on_luxid_connect` and the lazy fix-up | no SocialApp credentials locally, so the provider buttons and the OAuth round trip were not rendered or executed; LuxID claims were checked only against code (consistent: "goes live on submit" is the lazy fix-up) |
| Real email delivery, spam placement, link expiry in practice | rendered every member-facing email path I touched; checked expiry settings (verification link 3 days, reset 72 h) | no real mail transport; "email never arrives" was reasoned from the page, resend cooldown and rate limits, not observed |
| Phone OTP (SMS/WhatsApp) | read the modal and views; rendered the phone step | no Firebase/Meta credentials, so no real code was sent; WhatsApp OTP button is hidden locally (`whatsapp_otp_enabled` false) |
| Wizard behaviour in a real browser (autosave timing, canton map, photo upload, HTMX states, keyboard overlap, one-handed reach) | rendered all four steps as text; read the Alpine gates (`core.js`) | Playwright was used only for 390x844 geometry on four pages (static files proxied because the dev server 404s `/static/`); I did not drive the wizard or upload photos |
| Screen reader / focus order / contrast | read labels, `aria-*`, `sr-only` headings; measured tap targets | no screen-reader run, no contrast measurement; findings of type `accessibility` are therefore absent. Not filed: wizard step 1 **Continue** is disabled without an inline reason when no gender is chosen (`core.js:3704`); only the phone case has an amber explanation |
| Push (web/iOS/Android) delivery and the permission prompt timing | read `notification_service.py`, `push_notifications.py`, `_push_activation_prompt.html`, `pwa-install.js` | no push subscription, no PWA install, no iOS device; install/push claims are from code (XC-24 marked medium confidence) |
| WhatsApp templates (Connect Week request, campaigns) | read the senders and the consent gates | Meta credentials unset locally; template wording lives in Meta, not in the repo, so I could not review it |
| Emails I did not render | organiser-cancel, payment-refunded, curated-group trio, recap, feedback request, Connect-week/beta/catalogue, invitations, journey gift, receipts, pre-screening/screening trio, newsletters | belong to other products (SD/QN/CC/coach/payments), are staff/legacy, or need heavy context; sender functions and gates were read and listed in Section 3.5 section 4 |
| Event registration / checkout (SumUp) and payments | read the cancellation policy partial, `cancellation_outcome`, credit service, checkout template | payments are another product and a no-edit zone; I did not create PaymentTransactions or open a checkout |
| Premium recovery, `profile_submitted`, `_verification_*`, dashboard.html | listed in the journey, not re-reviewed | owned by open PR #1163; where my findings touch these files I say "coordinate" |
| Do-not-touch items | not re-reported: #925/#1164, #1165 (dashboard install-card cooldown, a11y), #1166 (ungated LuxID claims, recap strings, "Salut" greetings), #1167/#1058 (legacy delete route, contact-choices, DE "points available", `confirm()` leftovers), #1054 ("blur" copy), #1147, #1154, #1153, #1168, #1055, #1175, #1176, #1126, #1012 | cited as `related_issue` only where my finding is adjacent |
| Coach panel, Crush Connect hub/wizard, quiz, speed-dating registration forms, event lobby | out of scope for XC | other reviewers' products |
| Languages | EN/DE/FR rendered for signup, login, reset, dashboard, nav, emails (welcome, reminders, registration, waitlist, payment-pending, cancellation, reminder) | the wizard, welcome, phone and coach-intro steps were rendered in EN only; DE/FR for those come from the .po audit (empty/fuzzy/identical-to-EN scan of every msgid used in my scope found only the few items in XC-20) |
| Personas | P1 and P2 walked on every step I rendered; P3 covered for unverified, expired link, wrong language, rejected, waitlisted, pending payment, cancelled, rate-limited | not reproduced: payment failed mid-checkout, slow connection (no throttling), screen reader, cookies blocked |

#### States I could not reproduce locally (and how I handled them)
- "Verified by LuxID" end-to-end - no provider; used the code path in `signals.py:3147-3230`.
- A real door rejection - created an `xc_rejected` profile directly (`verification_status='rejected'`); the stored reason was read from `views_checkin.py`, not from data.
- Late-cancel (<48 h) member flow - the cancel page and email variants were rendered via the service functions; the live cancel POST was not run (it moves money state).
- Organiser-cancel credit (EUR 20 vs EUR 15.50) - read from `services/credits.py` comments and constants, not executed.

### 7.6 Whole-review limits

- Phase 4 fixes were limited to copy/template text; no model, migration, payment, auth or permission change was made, and none is made by this PR.
- The Premium payment-recovery work (#1163, #925, #1164), curated application copy (#1175) and coach roster counts (#1176) are open and were not duplicated.
- Product-owner decisions are needed before several proposals can ship (Section 6, tag C).

## Appendix A: every finding in full

The one-sentence customer impact of each finding is in the Section 3 tables; this appendix adds the evidence, the reviewer's proposal and the verifier's corrections, trimmed where long (Hurts/Blocks keep more than Polish). DE member copy is informal (du), FR is formal (vous). Where a verifier marked the proposed change unsafe, use its notes, not the reviewer's AFTER copy.
